[C.R.C.]{.abbr title="Consolidated Regulations of Canada"}, c. 1605

[WEIGHTS AND MEASURES ACT](/eng/acts/W-6)

Weights and Measures Regulations

s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-532538 .Part}

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** In these Regulations,

[[acceptance limits of error]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[acceptance limits of error]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the limits
    of error that apply to a device when the performance of the device
    is tested

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the time the class, type or design of that
      device is evaluated for approval;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the time the device is examined before its
      first use in trade;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} at the time the measuring elements of the device
      are overhauled or repaired following the failure of the device on
      examination to measure within the applicable limits of error; or

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} at any time within 30 days after the time
      referred to in paragraph (b) or (c); ([marge de tolérance à
      l'acceptation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Weights and Measures
    Act](/eng/acts/W-6); ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[automatic weighing device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[automatic weighing device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[ \[Repealed,
    SOR/2005-130, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[Canadian unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Canadian unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a unit of measurement
    set out in Schedule II to the Act; ([unité
    canadienne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[examination mark]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[examination mark]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a mark that indicates
    the year in which a device was examined; ([marque
    d'examen]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[examination sticker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[examination sticker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a self-adhesive
    label that indicates the month and year in which a device was
    examined and, if applicable, the month and year of the next
    examination required under section 15 of the Act, as determined in
    accordance with section 29; ([étiquette d'examen]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[height]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[height]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, with respect to the height of
    a letter, the height of an upper case letter where words appear in
    upper case and the height of the lower case letter "o" where words
    appear in lower case or in a mixture of upper and lower
    case; ([hauteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[in-service limits of error]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[in-service limits of error]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the limits
    of error that apply to a device when the performance of the device
    is tested at any time other than a time referred to in the
    definition of [[acceptance limits of
    error]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}; ([marge de tolérance en
    service]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[inspection mark]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[inspection mark]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2014-111, s.
    1\]]{.Repealed}

[[Measurement Canada office]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Measurement Canada office]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any office
    of the Department of Industry for use by persons employed in the
    administration of the Act; ([bureau de Mesures
    Canada]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[measuring tank]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[measuring tank]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a tank for use in trade
    for measuring the volume of liquid received or delivered, and
    includes a fixed, portable or vehicle-mounted tank; ([réservoir
    jaugeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[metric unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[metric unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a unit of measurement set
    out in Schedule I to the Act; ([unité métrique]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[non-automatic weighing device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[non-automatic weighing device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[ \[Repealed,
    SOR/2005-130, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[packing device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[packing device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device that, as part
    of a mechanical packaging system, measures a predetermined quantity
    of commodity without recording the measurement of each quantity of
    commodity measured by the device or without being operated by a
    person who observes or records the measurement of each quantity of
    commodity measured by the device; ([instrument
    d'emballage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[previous enactment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[previous enactment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any Act of
    Parliament relating to weights and measures, and any regulations
    made under that Act, enacted prior to the [Weights and Measures
    Act](/eng/acts/W-6), S.C. 1970-71-72, c. 36; ([texte législatif
    antérieur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[standard quantity commodity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[standard quantity commodity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    commodity that is part of a lot made up of the same commodity, with
    identical net quantity statements; ([marchandise à quantité
    standard]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[tank]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[tank]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2014-111, s.
    1\]]{.Repealed}

[[vehicle tank]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[vehicle tank]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a measuring tank that is
    mounted on a vehicle other than a vehicle for railway
    use. ([réservoir sur véhicule]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[weights and measures office]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[weights and measures office]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[ \[Repealed,
    SOR/2005-297, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
- SOR/90-118, s. 1
- SOR/93-234, s. 2
- SOR/98-115, s. 1
- SOR/2005-130, s. 1
- SOR/2005-297, ss. 1, 41(F)
- SOR/2014-111, ss. 1, 47(F)
:::

::: PITLink

Version](section-2-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 2"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART I]{.HLabel1}[Devices]{.HTitleText1} {#h-532565 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532566 .Subheading}

**[[2.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.1 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part and in Schedule I.

[[downstream petroleum trade]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[downstream petroleum trade]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the trade
    of refined petroleum products and liquid alternative fuels between
    petroleum refineries, gas processing facilities, wholesalers,
    retailers and commercial customers. ([commerce pétrolier
    aval]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[fishing products]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fishing products]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means finfish, shellfish,
    marine vegetation and marine animals, including seals but no other
    furry marine animals, that are obtained from ocean or inland waters
    or aquaculture farms, and the products and by-products of their
    processing. ([produits de la pêche]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[forestry products]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[forestry products]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} trees, logs and firewood;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} wood by-products other than methyl alcohol and
      wood resins; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} paper, other than recycled paper in its untreated
      form. ([produits forestiers]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[grain and field crop products and services]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[grain and field crop products and
    services]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cereal grains, oil seeds, grain corn, wheat,
      forage, hay, dry field peas, beans and seeds for those crops;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} feed grains and other animal foods processed by
      feed mills; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} grain storage services offered in grain
      elevators. ([grains, grandes cultures et services
      connexes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[mining products]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[mining products]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means metal and non-metal
    ores, refined ores and their transformed products, as well as
    refined precious metals and precious stones, but does not include
    scrap metal, products produced from the subsequent refining of
    precious metals and precious stones and, if used for construction,
    sand, gravel, stone and concrete products. ([produits
    miniers]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[retail food trade]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[retail food trade]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the retail trade of
    food by retail food stores, including grocery stores, supermarkets,
    delicatessens, ethnic food stores, natural food stores, butcher
    shops, farmers' markets, fish and seafood retailers, fruit and
    vegetable retailers, coffee retailers and specialty candy and nuts
    retailers. ([commerce au détail des aliments]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[retail petroleum trade]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[retail petroleum trade]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the retail
    trade of liquid refined petroleum products and liquid alternative
    fuels. ([commerce pétrolier au détail]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 2
:::

### [Exemptions from the Act]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532583 .Subheading}

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A class,
  type or design of device is exempt from the Act where the class, type
  or design is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subject to verification pursuant to the
    [Electricity and Gas Inspection Act](/eng/acts/E-4); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} designed for use and used only in laboratories or
    in scientific study and not approved pursuant to section 3 of the
    Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A device is exempt from the Act if it is used
  exclusively for

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} gauging and strapping tanker ship holds or tanks
    that have a capacity of more than 55,000 litres and that are used in
    the downstream petroleum trade; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} gauging ship holds that are used in the wholesale
    trade of mining products.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/80-429, s. 1
- SOR/93-234, s. 2
- SOR/2014-111, s. 3
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 3"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Exemptions from Section 8 and Subsection 15(1) of the Act]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532593 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/90-118, s. 2
- SOR/2014-111, s. 4

\]
:::

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following classes or types of devices are exempt from section 8 and
  subsection 15(1) of the Act:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} water meters;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} parking meters;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} taxi meters;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} odometers of motor vehicles;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} coin-operated machines for weighing persons;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} payment-operated machines that dispense a
    predetermined quantity of liquid and that do not register by a
    mechanical or an electrical indicator the quantity of liquid
    dispensed;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} clocks, watches, chronometers and other time
    measuring devices;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} milk measuring tanks for farm use;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} measuring tanks mounted on railway cars;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} measuring tanks that have a capacity in excess of
    55 000 L or 12,000 gallons;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} meters for the measurement of grease and other
    commodities having flow properties similar to grease;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} static measures that are used as containers in
    which a commodity is sold or offered for sale;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} packing devices;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} weighing machines designed for use in laboratories,
    scientific study or weighing precious metals and that are used for
    weighing precious metals or other commodities of comparable value,
    if they conform to applicable specifications for performance,
    installation and use established by these Regulations and if, prior
    to December 31, 1981, they are examined and certified as meeting
    such specifications;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} linear static measures; and

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} devices used in the downstream petroleum trade to
    measure liquids carried in a permanent closed piping system, either
    to or from a place where they are processed, treated or stored,
    other than to a place where the product is consumed or processed and
    is no longer considered a hydrocarbon.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A device is exempt from section 8 and subsection
  15(1) of the Act if it is used exclusively to weigh or measure

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} standard quantity commodities; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} crude petroleum products.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/80-429, s. 2
- SOR/90-118, s. 3
- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2005-297, ss. 2, 41(F)
- SOR/2014-111, ss. 5, 45, 46, 48(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-4-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 4"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Exemptions from Paragraph 8(a) and Subsection 15(1) of the Act]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532619 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/93-234, s. 2
- SOR/2014-111, s. 6

\]
:::

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** A measuring tank is exempt
from paragraph 8(a) and subsection 15(1) of the Act if it conforms to
applicable specifications relating to design, composition, construction
and performance set out in Divisions I, V, X and XII of Part V and any
additional specifications established under section 13.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/90-118, s. 4
- SOR/2014-111, s. 6
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-5-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 5"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Exemptions from Paragraph 8(b) and Subsection 15(1) of the Act]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532625 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/93-234, s. 2
- SOR/94-691, s. 4(E)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 6

\]
:::

**[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** A device is exempt from
paragraph 8(b) and subsection 15(1) of the Act if it is used exclusively
in the wholesale trade of gold, silver or diamonds, after initial
refining.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/90-118, s. 4
- SOR/2014-111, s. 6
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-6-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 6"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Exemptions from Paragraph 8(b), Subsection 15(1) and Paragraph 26(1)(c) of the Act]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532631 .Subheading}

- **[[6.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a meter
  register approved under section 3 of the Act that has no means, or has
  means that are not in use, of automatic temperature, pressure or
  density compensation is exempt from paragraph 8(b), subsection 15(1)
  and paragraph 26(1)(c) of the Act if it is installed as a replacement
  register on a volumetric liquid meter that has previously been
  examined and certified for the purposes of paragraph 8(b) of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a register that is
  altered to operate with automatic temperature, pressure or density
  compensation.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 6
:::

### [Exemptions from Section 8, Subsection 15(1), Section 23, Paragraph 24(b) and Section 33 of the Act]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532637 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2014-111, s. 7

\]
:::

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsection (2), section 8, subsection 15(1), section 23, paragraph
  24(b) and section 33 of the Act do not apply in respect of a trade
  transaction if the parties give written notice to the Minister that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a device approved for use in trade or approved for
    use in the particular manner or for the particular purpose
    contemplated by them is not available to them for use in their
    intended trade transaction;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} they wish to use in their intended trade
    transaction a device other than one referred to in paragraph (a);
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it would be impractical or unreasonably expensive
    to obtain approval for the class, type or design of device that they
    wish to use in their intended trade transaction or to comply with
    the examination requirement under paragraph 8(b) or subsection 15(1)
    of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A trade transaction referred to in subsection (1) is
  not exempt from the provisions of the Act referred to in that
  subsection unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the parties referred to in subsection (1) furnish
    to the Minister such information as the Minister requires relating
    to the device that the parties wish to use;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the device that the parties wish to use conforms to
    all requirements of the Act and these Regulations, except the
    specifications relating to design, composition, construction and
    performance;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the parties have entered into a written contract
    not exceeding three years in duration respecting the use of the
    device that the parties wish to use in the intended trade
    transaction showing

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the limits of error applicable to the device and
      to the commodities to be measured by the device,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the means to be used for calibrating the device,
      and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the intervals at which that device will be
      calibrated;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the parties have forwarded to the Minister a true
    copy of the part of the contract dealing with the use of the device;
    and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the device that the parties wish to use measures
    within the limits of error referred to in subparagraph (c)(i).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
- SOR/2005-297, s. 41(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 8
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-7-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 7"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Exemptions from Approval]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532656 .Subheading}

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to section 10, a device that has been examined and certified under a
  previous enactment but not approved pursuant to section 3 of the Act
  is exempt from paragraph 8(a) of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 10, a device or class, type or
  design of device that has been approved for use in trade under a
  previous enactment is exempt from paragraph 8(a) of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/90-118, s. 5
- SOR/2014-111, s. 46
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-8-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 8"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsection (2), where a device or class, type or design of device
  approved pursuant to subsection 3(1) of the Act or under a previous
  enactment, or a device examined and certified by an inspector on or
  before December 31, 1989, is altered to measure in terms of any units
  of measurement set out and defined in Schedule I of the Act, the
  device or class, type or design of device is exempt from paragraph
  8(a) of the Act if the device or class, type or design of device as
  altered conforms to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} applicable specifications relating to design,
    composition, construction and performance set out in Part V and any
    additional specifications established pursuant to section 13; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} applicable specifications relating to design,
    composition, construction and performance in force at the time the
    device was initially examined and certified by an inspector or when
    the device or class, type or design of device was approved.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of any
  device or class, type or design of device approved on a temporary
  basis pursuant to subsection 3(2) of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/90-118, s. 5
- SOR/2014-111, s. 46
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-9-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 9"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** A static measure examined
and certified by an inspector on or before December 31, 1989 is exempt
from paragraph 8(a) of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/90-118 s. 5
- SOR/2014-111, s. 46
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 10"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[10.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.1 .sectionLabel}** A device or class,
type or design of device is exempt from paragraph 8(a) of the Act if
every part of the device that can have an effect on the accuracy of the
device or class, type or design of device is approved pursuant to
section 3 of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/90-118, s. 5
:::

### [Exemptions from Subsection 19(2) of the Act]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532675 .Subheading}

**[[10.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.2 .sectionLabel}** The following devices
are exempt from subsection 19(2) of the Act:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} static measures; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} weights that do not have lead plugs that have been
  marked in accordance with paragraph 30(3)(d).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 9
:::

### [Exemption from Paragraph 26(1)(b) of the Act]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532681 .Subheading}

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** Since January 1, 1976,
static measures that are not for use in trade are exempt from paragraph
26(1)(b) of the Act.

### [Notice of Importation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532683 .Subheading}

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  classes, types or designs of devices described in subsection 4(1) are
  exempt from subsection 26(2) of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notice to the Minister referred to in subsection
  26(2) of the Act shall be in writing, shall be directed to the nearest
  Measurement Canada office within 10 days after the device is imported
  and shall contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and Canadian address of the importer;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date of entry of the device into Canada;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the port of entry of the device;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a description of the device sufficient to enable an
    inspector to identify it;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if more than one device is imported, the number of
    devices imported;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the address at which the device may be examined;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} whether the device is of a class, type or design
    approved for use in trade, and if so, the approval number referred
    to in paragraph 17(c) for that class, type or design of device; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} whether the device has been marked in accordance
    with these Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2005-297, ss. 40, 41(F), 43
- SOR/2014-111, s. 46
:::

### [Specifications Relating to Approval]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532697 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)

\]
:::

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  addition to the specifications set out in Part V, the Minister may
  establish specifications relating to the design, composition,
  construction and performance to which any device or class, type or
  design of device shall conform before it may be approved pursuant to
  section 3 of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2005-297, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A device or class, type or design of device is
  exempted from the application of any or all of the provisions of the
  specifications set out in Part V or established under subsection (1)
  if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the provision or provisions are inapplicable or
    inappropriate taking into account

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the distinctive characteristics of the design,
      composition and construction of the device or class, type or
      design of device, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the use for which the device or class, type or
      design of device is to be approved pursuant to section 3 of the
      Act; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the device or class, type or design of device
    provides a degree of accuracy and security that is at least
    equivalent to the degree provided by a device or class, type or
    design of device that meets the requirements of the provision or
    provisions of the specifications.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a device or class, type or design of device is
  exempted from the application of any or all of the provisions of the
  specifications set out in Part V or established under subsection (1),
  the exemption shall be set out in the notice of approval issued
  pursuant to section 3 of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-792, s. 1
- SOR/90-278, s. 1
- SOR/93-234, s. 2
- SOR/2005-297, s. 3
:::

### [Application for Approval of a Class, Type or Design of Device]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532711 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2005-297, s. 41(F)

\]
:::

**[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** An application for the
approval of a device, or of a class, type or design of device for use in
trade shall be made in writing to the Minister and shall contain the
following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the commodity that a device of that
  class, type or design is designed to measure;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the different units of measurement registered by a
  device of that class, type or design;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the range of flow rate or capacity of a device of
  that class, type or design; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} where the manner of installation or use affects the
  performance of a device of that class, type or design, the manner in
  which the device is to be installed or used.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/85-736, s. 1
- SOR/2005-297, s. 41(F)
:::

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  application referred to in section 14 shall be accompanied by
  duplicate copies of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} test data relating to the actual service
    performance of the device or to the simulated service performance of
    the device;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the principal drawings and specifications to be
    used by the manufacturer in assembling, testing and, where
    applicable, installing devices of that class, type or design; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the promotional material, bulletins, brochures,
    instructions and other information distributed or intended for
    distribution to purchasers or prospective purchasers of a device of
    the class, type or design to which the application relates.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who submits an application under section 14
  shall, when requested to do so by the Minister, supply to the Minister
  such additional information, test data, drawings or specifications as
  are necessary to enable the Minister to deal with the application.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-297, s. 41(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 10(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-15-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 15"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** On the request of the
Minister and under any conditions that may be specified by him or her,
the applicant shall make available to the Minister one or more devices
of the class, type or design to which the application relates, together
with any information, equipment, material and services that are required
to evaluate the device for approval.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-297, s. 41(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 11
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 16"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Notice of Approval]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532732 .Subheading}

**[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** Where the Minister
approves a class, type or design of device for use in trade, he shall
issue to the applicant therefor a written notice of approval containing
the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the class, type or design of device
  approved;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date of approval;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the approval number assigned to the class, type or
  design of device approved;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} where a class, type or design of device is approved
  for use in trade for a specific purpose only, the specific purpose for
  which it may be used and the purpose for which its use is prohibited;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} where a class, type or design of device is approved
  for use in trade in a specific manner only, the specific manner in
  which it may be used and the manner in which its use is prohibited;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} such information relating to specifications for
  installation or use of a device of the class, type or design approved
  as the Minister considers necessary;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the markings that are required to be shown on a
  device of that class, type or design; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} such other information as the Minister considers
  necessary.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-297, s. 41(F)
:::

### [Form of Marking of Devices]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532744 .Subheading}

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} With
  the exception of devices to which the Specifications Relating to
  Non-automatic Weighing Devices (1998) or the Specifications Relating
  to Timber Dimension Measuring Devices apply, the information required
  by these Regulations to be marked on a device shall be marked

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the device;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on a plate or plates permanently affixed to the
    device;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} partly on the device and partly on a plate or
    plates that are permanently affixed to the device; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a device not large enough to
    accommodate the required information in the manner referred to in
    paragraph (a), (b) or (c), on a notice attached to or displayed in
    close proximity to the device.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If information is marked on a plate, a suitable area
  of not less than 12 mm by 25 mm shall be left blank on the plate for
  the examination mark referred to in section 30 and the plate shall be
  made of a durable material and shall be located and mounted so that it
  can be stamped with a steel die.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The information required by these Regulations to be
  marked on the device shall be shown in numbers or letters that are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} permanent;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} clear;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} of a height reasonably appropriate to the size of
    the device and, unless otherwise specified in these Regulations or
    in specifications established by the Minister, in numbers or letters
    of not less than 3 mm or 1/8 inch in height; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} located in such a place as to be readily legible by
    a person using the device under normal conditions of use.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} When numbers are used with letters or in place of
  letters on any marking on a device, the size and height of the numbers
  shall correspond to the size and height of the letters used or
  required to be used thereon by these Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/98-115, s. 2
- SOR/2005-130, s. 2
- SOR/2014-111, s. 12
- [SOR/2024-183, s. 1](#1488100-1488639){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1488100-1488639.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-18-20140801.html "Link to previous version of section 18"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Marking Static Measures Approved for Use in Trade]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532760 .Subheading}

**[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** Before being sold or
otherwise disposed of, or leased, a static measure that is of a class,
type or design approved for use in trade and that is manufactured on or
after January 1, 1976 shall be marked with the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name or registered trademark of the manufacturer
  or importer of the measure;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the approval number for that class, type or design of
  measure;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the capacity of the measure expressed in terms of the
  appropriate unit of measurement;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the words "Legal for Trade" ("[Légal pour le
  commerce]{.otherLang lang="fr"}"); and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} any other information required to be marked on the
  device by the notice of approval.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2005-297, s. 41(F); 2014, c. 20, s. 366(E); SOR/2014-111, s.
  13](#1044114-1145774){.wb-lbx aria-controls="centred-popup"
  ajax-after="1044114-1145774.html" role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19-20140801.html "Link to previous version of section 19"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Marking Weights Approved for Use in Trade]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532769 .Subheading}

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before
  being sold or otherwise disposed of, or leased, a weight that is of a
  class, type or design approved for use in trade shall be marked with
  the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of units of mass or weight that the
    weight contains;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a counterpoise weight used as part
    of a weighing machine, the number of units of mass or weight to
    which the weight is equivalent when used as a counterpoise with the
    weighing machine of which it forms a part;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a weight of 1 gram or multiple or
    submultiple thereof, the word "gram" or its symbol "g", together
    with the appropriate prefix or symbol as set out in Part V of
    Schedule I to the Act;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a weight of 1 troy ounce, or
    multiple or submultiple thereof, the word "troy" or its
    abbreviation; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a weight of any avoirdupois unit or
    multiple or submultiple thereof that is manufactured or imported
    after January 1, 1976, the name of the unit or the symbol or
    abbreviation for the unit.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs (1)(c), (d) and (e) do not apply to a
  weight that is so small that it is impracticable to show the
  information required by those paragraphs.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(a) does not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} sheet metal or wire weights of 1 milligram or
    multiples or submultiples thereof, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sheet metal or wire weights of 1 grain or multiples
    or submultiples thereof

  if the weights are manufactured in the customary distinctive shapes to
  denote the multiple or submultiple of the milligram or grain that the
  weight represents.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-297, s. 41(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 14
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-20-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 20"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Marking Approved Weighing Machines, Measuring Machines and Related Equipment and Accessories]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532784 .Subheading}

- **[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before
  being sold or otherwise disposed of, or leased, a weighing machine or
  measuring machine that is of a class, type or design approved under
  section 3 of the Act, and any equipment or accessory attached to or
  used in conjunction with the machine that has or could have an effect
  on the accuracy of the machine and that was approved under section 3
  of the Act, shall be marked with the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the manufacturer or importer of the
    machine, equipment or accessory;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the model number and serial number of the machine,
    equipment or accessory;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the approval number of the machine, equipment or
    accessory;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a volumetric liquid meter,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the minimum and maximum flow rates, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the meter is equipped with an automatic
      temperature compensator, the words "Volume corrected to 15°C" or
      "[Volume corrigé à 15°C]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", immediately
      adjacent to the registration of net quantity;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a weighing machine, the maximum load
    that may be weighed on the machine;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any equipment or accessory, the
    range for which it was approved pursuant to section 3 of the Act;
    and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} any other information that is required by the
    notice of approval issued pursuant to section 3 of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to weighing machines or
  measuring machines to which the Specifications Relating to
  Non-automatic Weighing Devices (1998) or the Specifications Relating
  to Timber Dimension Measuring Devices apply.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/90-118, s. 6
- SOR/98-115, s. 3
- SOR/2005-130, s. 3
- SOR/2014-111, s. 15
- [SOR/2024-183, s. 2](#1488102-1488659){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1488102-1488659.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-21-20140801.html "Link to previous version of section 21"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** Before being sold or
otherwise disposed of, or leased, a weighing machine or measuring
machine that is of a class, type or design approved under section 3 of
the Act and that is referred to in subsection 28(2) shall be marked "Not
for Use in Trade until Examined" ("[Ne pas utiliser dans le commerce
avant l'examen]{.otherLang lang="fr"}") or shall be marked with words
that convey the same meaning, and those markings shall be in letters of
not less than 12 mm in height and shall remain on the device until it
has been examined and is found to meet the requirements of the Act and
these Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2005-297, s. 41(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 16
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-22-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 22"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before
  being sold or otherwise disposed of, or leased, a weighing machine or
  measuring machine that is not of a class, type or design approved
  under section 3 of the Act and that is not exempt from approval under
  that section shall be marked "Not Legal for Trade" ("[Non légal pour
  le commerce]{.otherLang lang="fr"}") in letters of not less than 6 mm
  in height.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A device described in paragraph 4(2)(a) shall be
  marked "For Prepackaging Use Only" ("[Pour préemballage
  seulement]{.otherLang lang="fr"}") in letters of not less than 6 mm in
  height.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-234, s. 2
- SOR/2005-297, s. 4
- SOR/2014-111, s. 16
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-23-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 23"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Marking Measuring Tanks]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532805 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2014-111, s. 48(F)

\]
:::

- **[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before
  being sold or otherwise disposed of, or leased, a measuring tank that
  is of a class, type or design approved under section 3 of the Act
  shall be marked with the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the manufacturer or importer of the
    measuring tank;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the model number and serial number of the measuring
    tank;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the approval number for that class, type or design
    of measuring tank;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the maximum capacity of the measuring tank;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a measuring tank that contains one
    or more internal capacity indicators, the capacity of the measuring
    tank at the level of each indicator and where there is more than one
    indicator, the various capacities shown in descending order;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a measuring tank that has been
    calibrated to a valve located immediately at the outlet of the
    measuring tank or that has been calibrated to an emergency valve,
    the words "dry line calibration" ("[calibré avec tuyauterie
    vide]{.otherLang lang="fr"}");

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a measuring tank that has not been
    calibrated as set out in paragraph (f), the words "wet line
    calibration" ("[calibré avec tuyauterie pleine]{.otherLang
    lang="fr"}"); and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} in the case of two or more measuring tanks
    installed on a vehicle, a number or letter in an identifiable
    sequence commencing with the tank closest to the front of the
    vehicle and proceeding to the tank closest to the rear of the
    vehicle.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where two or more measuring tanks are installed on a
  vehicle, the information required by paragraphs (1)(a) to (g) may
  appear in one location if the tanks are identified in accordance with
  paragraph (1)(h) and the information is correlated to the number or
  letter shown on the tank.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The information required by paragraphs (1)(f), (g)
  and (h) shall be shown in numbers or letters of not less than 12 mm or
  ½ inch in height.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Every internal capacity indicator in a measuring tank
  shall be marked in numbers or letters at least 6 mm or ¼ inch in
  height

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to show the capacity of the tank at the level of
    the indicator; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to identify the indicator.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where the internal capacity indicator is identified
  in accordance with paragraph (4)(b), the identification and the
  capacity of the measuring tank at the level of that indicator shall be
  shown on the outside of the neck or fill opening of the measuring tank
  in numbers or letters of at least 12 mm or ½ inch in height.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} When two or more measuring tanks are installed on a
  vehicle, the discharge valve for each tank shall be marked in numbers
  or letters of not less than 6 mm or ¼ inch in height to show the tank
  from which the commodity is discharged.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-297, s. 41(F)
- SOR/2014-111, ss. 17, 45, 48(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-24-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 24"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Marking Devices "Not for Use in Trade"]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532827 .Subheading}

**[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** Before being sold or
otherwise disposed of, or leased, for a use other than use in trade, a
weighing machine or measuring machine that is of a class, type or design
approved under section 3 of the Act shall be permanently marked "Not for
Use in Trade" ("[Ne pas utiliser dans le commerce]{.otherLang
lang="fr"}") in letters of not less than 12 mm in height.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/90-118, s. 7
- SOR/2014-111, s. 18
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-25-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 25"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** Where a trader has in his
possession any device that is of a class, type or design approved for
use in trade but that is not in his possession for use in trade, the
trader shall, for the purpose of section 36 of the Act, mark the device
"Not for Use in Trade" ("[Ne pas utiliser dans le commerce]{.otherLang
lang="fr"}") or shall mark the device in words that convey the same
meaning and the marking shall be in letters of not less than 12 mm or ½
inch in height.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-297, s. 41(F)
:::

### [Specifications Relating to Installation and Use]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532834 .Subheading}

**[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** In addition to the
specifications set out in Part V, the Minister may from time to time
establish specifications relating to the installation and use of any
device or class, type or design of device for use in trade.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-792, s. 2
- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
:::

### [Examination of Devices]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532838 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/93-234, s. 2
- SOR/2014-111, s. 19

\]
:::

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of paragraph 26(1)(c) of the Act and subject to
  subsections (2) and (3), a device that is of a class, type or design
  approved under section 3 of the Act or that is described in section 8,
  other than a static measure or a device described in subsection 4(2),
  shall not be sold or otherwise disposed of, or leased, unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the device is manufactured in Canada and the
    manufacturer has had it examined by an inspector; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the device is imported into Canada and the dealer
    who imported the device has had it examined by an inspector.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to any device, the
  performance of which cannot be examined until the device is installed
  for use in trade, if the dealer

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} marks the device in the manner set out in section
    22;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} within five days after shipping the device, sends
    to the nearest Measurement Canada office a notice in writing
    containing

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the dealer's name and address,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person to whom the device was
      shipped,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the device, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the address of the place where the device is to
      be installed; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} advises the trader that the device must be examined
    by an inspector before it is used in trade.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a device
  that is to be sold or otherwise disposed of, or leased, for a use
  other than use in trade if the dealer

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} marks the device in the manner set out in section
    25; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} within five days after selling or otherwise
    disposing of, or leasing, the device, sends to the nearest
    Measurement Canada office a notice in writing containing

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the dealer's name and address,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person to whom the
      device was sold or otherwise disposed of, or leased, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the device, including the
      approval number, serial number and model number.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/90-118, s. 9
- SOR/93-234, s. 2
- SOR/2005-297, ss. 5, 40, 41(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 19
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-28-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 28"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purpose of subsection 15(1) of the Act, a trader who uses or
  possesses for use a device set out in column I of Part I of Schedule
  I, in the category of trade set out in column II, shall cause the
  device to be examined within the period set out in column III,
  counting from the day on which the most recent certificate showing
  that the device meets the requirements of the Act and these
  Regulations was issued following the examination referred to in

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 8(b) of the Act, before the first use in
    trade of the device; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} section 15 or 15.1 of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a device falls under more than one item of Part I
  of Schedule I, the shortest period set out in column III for those
  items applies for the purposes of subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 19
- SOR/2017-198, s. 3
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-29-20140801.html "Link to previous version of section 29"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[29.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
subsection 15(2) of the Act, the Minister may grant to a trader an
extension of a period established under subsection 29(1) if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the trader sends to the nearest Measurement Canada
  office a written request containing

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the trader's name and address,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the address where the device is located, if
    different from that referred to in subparagraph (i),

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the device's make, model and serial number,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the range of the flow rate or the maximum load
    that may be measured by the device,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the date of the most recent regulatory examination
    of the device,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} a description of the purpose for which the device
    is used, sufficient to determine the category of trade in which it
    is used,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the reason for the extension request,

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the efforts made to comply with the period, and

  - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} the duration of the extension being requested, not
    to exceed one year;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the trader establishes that circumstances beyond
  their control make it impossible to have the device examined within
  the period; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the trader has not previously been granted an
  extension of the period.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 19
- SOR/2017-198, s. 4
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
