### [Marking of Devices]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532883 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 19

\]
:::

- **[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  inspector who, for the purposes of paragraph 8(b) or 26(1)(c) of the
  Act, examines a device and determines that it meets the requirements
  of the Act and these Regulations shall mark the device or the
  container in which the device is stored when not in use with an
  examination mark in accordance with subsections (2), (3) and (4).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A device referred to in subsection (1) shall be
  marked by means of a steel die or, if that is not possible, by means
  of a sticker.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The examination mark shall be placed,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a weighing or measuring machine to
    which a plate is permanently affixed, on the blank area referred to
    in subsection 18(2) or, if that is not possible, close to the plate;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a weighing or measuring machine to
    which no plate is permanently affixed, on a part of the machine
    where it will be readily legible to a person using the machine under
    normal conditions of use;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a weight that has a lead plug, on
    the lead plug; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a weight that does not have a lead
    plug, on any appropriate part of the weight.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (3), if a device is so small that
  it cannot be marked in accordance with that subsection, the
  examination mark shall be placed on the container in which the device
  is stored when not in use.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 19
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-30-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 30"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[30.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30.1 .sectionLabel}** An inspector who
marks a weighing or measuring machine with an examination mark under
section 30 shall also place an examination sticker on the device.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 19
:::

- **[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  inspector who, under section 15 of the Act, examines a weighing or
  measuring machine set out in column I of Part I of Schedule I that is
  or is to be used in the category of trade set out in column II or who,
  under section 15.1 of the Act, examines a weighing or measuring
  machine that is or is to be used in trade and, in either case,
  determines that the machine meets the requirements of the Act and
  these Regulations shall mark the machine by placing on it an
  examination sticker.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An inspector who, under paragraph 17(1)(b) or
  subsection 21(2) of the Act, examines a weighing or measuring machine
  that is or is to be used in trade and determines that it meets the
  requirements of the Act and these Regulations shall mark the machine
  by placing on it a sticker bearing the examination mark.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An inspector who, under section 15 of the Act,
  examines a weight set out in column I of Part I of Schedule I that has
  a lead plug and that is or is to be used in the category of trade set
  out in column II or who, under section 15.1, paragraph 17(1)(b) or
  subsection 21(2) of the Act, examines a weight that has a lead plug
  that is or is to be used in trade and, in either case, determines that
  the weight meets the requirements of the Act and these Regulations
  shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} mark the plug with an examination mark by means of
    a steel die; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the weight is so small that this is not
    possible, place an examination mark on the container in which the
    weight is stored when not in use.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 19
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-31-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 31"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Sealing of Devices]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532908 .Subheading}

**[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
paragraph 19(2)(a) of the Act, the seal that shall be attached by an
inspector who marks, as prescribed by section 30 or 31, a volumetric
liquid meter, measuring tank or electronic device, other than a device
to which the Specifications Relating to Non-automatic Weighing Devices
(1998) or the Specifications Relating to Timber Dimension Measuring
Devices apply, shall be of the self-adhesive, self-locking or
lead-and-wire type.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/98-115, s. 4
- SOR/2005-130, s. 4
- SOR/2014-111, ss. 19, 48(F)
- [SOR/2024-183, s. 3](#1488107-1488662){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1488107-1488662.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-32-20140801.html "Link to previous version of section 32"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Accessibility and Assistance for Examining and Sealing Devices]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532912 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2014-111, s. 20

\]
:::

- **[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the owner
  or person in possession of a device shall ensure that the location of
  the device, or access to it by customary means, is such that an
  inspector is able

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to examine the device, including performing a
    thorough visual examination of it and its components;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to verify the sealing means on the device and to
    seal it; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to transport and to manoeuvre by customary means
    the local standards and other equipment necessary for performing the
    examination.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if the owner or person
  in possession of the device agrees

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to put at the inspector's disposal the necessary
    facilities and to supply the necessary labour, test products,
    standards calibrated in relation to a relevant local standard and
    any other equipment necessary for examining and sealing the device;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on the request of the inspector, to remove the
    device to a suitable location and to supply the necessary equipment,
    material and services for examining and sealing the device.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2005-130, s. 5\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/98-115, s. 4
- SOR/2005-130, s. 5
- SOR/2014-111, ss. 21, 49(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-33-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 33"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Device Maintenance and Adjustment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532926 .Subheading}

**[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** A device shall be
maintained in proper operating condition and, where cleanliness can
affect its performance, shall be kept clean.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/98-115, s. 4
:::

**[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** Where a device has a
means of adjustment, an adjustment shall be made only to correct the
condition that the adjustment is designed to correct and shall not be
used to compensate for faulty installation or for worn or defective
parts.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/98-115, s. 4
:::

**[[35.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35.1 .sectionLabel}** A person who alters,
adjusts or repairs a device in a way that may affect the accuracy of
measurement shall also take steps to ensure that the device is
calibrated immediately after the alteration, adjustment or repair so
that the measurement error is as close to zero as possible within the
normal operating range of the device.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/98-115, s. 4
:::

### [Rejected Static Measures]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532936 .Subheading}

**[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** Where an inspector
determines that a static measure that is or is to be used in trade does
not meet the requirements of the Act and these Regulations, he shall
deface any markings referred to in paragraphs 19(b) and (d).

**[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/90-118,
s. 11\]]{.Repealed}

### [Reporting Location of Devices Installed for Use in Trade]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532939 .Subheading}

- **[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  section and section 39 do not apply to a class or type of device
  referred to in subsection 4(1) or to a device used in a trade
  transaction referred to in section 7.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a trader installs or causes to be installed for
  use in trade

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a volumetric liquid measuring machine as part of a
    receiving or dispensing system, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other device that, before it is used, is
    installed on a base, foundation, frame or other means of support, or
    is incorporated into a structure or system specifically made or
    prepared for the device, and the manner of installation can affect
    the performance of the device,

  the trader shall, within five days after the date of the installation,
  make a report in writing to the nearest Measurement Canada office
  containing the following information:

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the trader;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the make, model and serial number of the device;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the range of flow rate or capacity of the device;
    and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the address and description of the place where the
    device is installed.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a device described in subsection (2) has been
  removed from its installation and is installed again, the trader who
  owns the device or has it in their possession for use in trade shall,
  within five days after the date of the installation, make a report in
  writing to the nearest Measurement Canada office containing the
  information required by subsection (2) and the address and description
  of the place where the device was previously installed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-297, ss. 40, 41(F), 43
- SOR/2014-111, s. 22(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-38-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 38"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a trader acquires a measuring machine for use in trade that is part of
  a receiving or dispensing system installed on a vehicle, he shall make
  a report in writing to the nearest Measurement Canada office within
  five days from the date of the acquisition containing the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the trader;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the make, model and serial number of the machine;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the range of flow rate or capacity of the machine;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the make, fleet and unit number of the vehicle on
    which the machine is installed;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the address, if known, at which the machine was
    located prior to the acquisition; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the new address at which the machine can be
    examined.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a trader who owns or has in their possession for
  use in trade a measuring machine described in subsection (1) changes
  the address at which the machine can be examined, they shall, within
  five days after the date of changing the address, make a report in
  writing to the nearest Measurement Canada office containing the
  information required by paragraphs (1)(a), (b), (c), (d) and (f) and
  the address at which the machine could have been examined before the
  change of address.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2005-297, s. 40
- SOR/2014-111, s. 23
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-39-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 39"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Written Notice Subsequent to Repairing Device]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532964 .Subheading}

- **[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  device that is of a class or type referred to in subsection 4(1) or a
  device used in a trade transaction referred to in section 7 is exempt
  from paragraphs 29(a) and (b) and section 30 of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The written notice referred to in subparagraph
  29(a)(ii) of the Act shall be forwarded to the nearest Measurement
  Canada office within five days from the date of the repair of the
  device and shall contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person who made the
    repair;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person to whom the
    repaired device will be sent;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if known to the person who made the repair, the
    address of the place where the device will be located for
    examination, if that address is different from the address referred
    to in paragraph (b);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the date of completion of the repair;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the make, model and serial number of the device;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the range of flow rate or capacity of the device;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a description of the repair made;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a description of the tests made after the repair to
    determine the accuracy of the device, the results of such tests and
    the equipment used for the testing;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/81-623, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} whether the person who made the repair removed or
    broke or found removed or broken any marking, tag or seal placed on
    or attached to the device by an inspector or a person who had
    previously adjusted, altered or repaired the device, and, where
    possible, a description of such marking, tag or seal; and

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} where the person who made the repair removed or
    broke or found removed or broken any seal, whether he replaced it
    with another seal and, if so, a description of that seal and the
    number of the seal, if any.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/81-623, s. 1
- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2005-297, ss. 40, 41(F), 43
- SOR/2014-111, ss. 46, 47(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-40-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 40"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Report Subsequent to Altering, Adjusting or Repairing Device]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532981 .Subheading}

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a person repairs a device that is or is to be used in trade without
  taking possession of it or adjusts or alters such a device, whether or
  not he takes possession of it, and the alteration, adjustment or
  repair is of a nature that may affect the accuracy of the device or
  may result in its no longer being of a class, type or design approved
  for use in trade, he shall, within five days after the date of the
  alteration, adjustment or repair, report the following information to
  the nearest Measurement Canada office:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} his name and address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person who has
    possession of the device;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the address of the place where the device is
    located if that address is different from the address referred to in
    paragraph (b);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the date of completion of the alteration,
    adjustment or repair;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the make, model and serial number of the device;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the range of flow rate or capacity of the device;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a description of the alteration, adjustment or
    repair made;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a description of the tests made after the
    alteration, adjustment or repair to determine the accuracy of the
    device, the results of such tests and the equipment used for such
    testing;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/81-623, s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} whether the person who made the alteration,
    adjustment or repair removed or broke or found removed or broken any
    marking, tag or seal placed on or attached to the device by an
    inspector or by a person who had previously altered, adjusted or
    repaired the device and, where possible, a description of such
    marking, tag or seal; and

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} where the person who made the alteration,
    adjustment or repair removed or broke or found removed or broken any
    seal, whether he replaced it with another seal and, if so, a
    description of that seal and the number of the seal, if any.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A report under subsection (1) shall be in writing,
  but an inspector may authorize the report to be made verbally, in
  which case the report shall be made verbally.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/81-623, s. 2
- SOR/2005-297, ss. 40, 41(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 47(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-41-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 41"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Reporting Removal of Marking or Tag or Breaking of Seals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-532998 .Subheading}

**[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** The report referred to in
paragraph 30(2)(b) of the Act shall be in writing, shall be forwarded to
the nearest Measurement Canada office within five days of the removal of
any marking or tag or breaking of any seal, or the discovery of the
removal of any marking or tag or breaking of any seal, and shall contain
the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person who has possession
  of the device;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the make, model and serial number of the device;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the range of flow rate or capacity of the device;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the marking or tag was removed or
  was discovered to have been removed;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the seal was broken or was
  discovered to have been broken;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the circumstances, if known, of the removal of the
  marking or tag or of the breaking of the seal;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} a description, where possible, of the marking or tag
  that was removed or discovered to have been removed, and of the seal
  that was broken or discovered to have been broken; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} a description of any seal and the number of the seal,
  if any, that was installed to replace the missing seal.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-297, s. 40
:::

### [Adjustments or Alterations Prescribed Pursuant to Section 16 of the Act]{.HTitleText2} {#h-533010 .Subheading}

**[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
section 16 of the Act, the adjustments or alterations that an inspector
may make to a device are such adjustments and alterations as are

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} minor in character and unlikely to require a
  significant amount of time to complete; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} needed to make the device comply with these
  Regulations and any additional specifications established pursuant to
  sections 13 and 27.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
- SOR/90-118, s. 12
:::

### [Limits of Error of Devices]{.HTitleText2} {#h-533016 .Subheading}

**[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** The limits of error of a
device for the purposes of paragraph 24(b) of the Act are those set out

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} as the in-service limits of error in the applicable
  table in Part V; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} as the in-service limits of error in the applicable
  specifications established under subsection 13(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
- SOR/2005-297, s. 6
:::

## [PART II]{.HLabel1}[Commodities and Services]{.HTitleText1} {#h-533022 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/90-278, s. 2

\]
:::

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-533025 .Subheading}

**[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** In this Part and in
Schedule II,

[[individually measured commodity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[individually measured commodity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    commodity that is measured and packaged in a manner other than in
    accordance with a predetermined fixed quantity and that is, as a
    result, sold in varying quantities; ([marchandise mesurée
    individuellement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[quantity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[quantity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the net quantity of a
    commodity; ([quantité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[statement of quantity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[statement of quantity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the quantity
    stated pursuant to section 9 of the Act. ([déclaration de
    quantité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 1
- SOR/93-234, s. 2
:::

### [Exemptions from Marking the Quantity of the Commodity]{.HTitleText2} {#h-533032 .Subheading}

**[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** The following types of
trade transactions are exempt from section 9 of the Act:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} sales or offers for sale of commodities that are
  weighed or measured in the presence of the purchaser at the time of
  sale;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} sales or offers for sale of commodities that are
  produced or manufactured only for export;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} sales or offers for sale by bakeries of bakery
  products that are not packaged before being offered for sale;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} retail sales or offers for sale of fresh fruits or
  fresh vegetables, if a sign or placard that shows the price per unit
  of measurement on which the total price of the commodity is based is
  displayed in close proximity to the commodity, and that are

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} not packaged before being offered for sale,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} packaged in a wrapper or confining band of less
    than 13 mm in width, or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} packaged in a clear, transparent protective
    wrapping;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} sales or offers for sale by automatic vending
  machines or mobile canteens of individual servings of food that are
  prepared by a commissary and packaged before being offered for sale;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} sales or offers for sale of individual portions of
  food that are packaged before being offered for sale and served by a
  restaurant or other commercial enterprise with meals or snacks;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} sales or offers for sale of individually wrapped
  confections, commonly known as one-bite confections, that are offered
  for sale or sold individually; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} sales or offers for sale of primary mill paper or
  primary paperboard products, where

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a statement of the net, gross or other quantity of
    the commodity and of the manner in which the quantity was calculated
    is shown in a legible manner on the side of the package containing
    the commodity that is visible to the purchaser under normal or
    customary conditions of sale or use, or on a shipping bill, bill of
    lading or other document accompanying the commodity, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} subject to section 49, the statement of quantity
    referred to in subparagraph (i) is accurate within the limits of
    error set out in Column II of an item of the appropriate Part of
    Schedule II for the stated quantity set out in Column I of that
    item.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/90-118, s. 13
- SOR/2005-297, s. 7
:::

### [Manner of Showing Statement of Quantity]{.HTitleText2} {#h-533049 .Subheading}

**[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** A commodity that is sold
or offered for sale on the basis of number or measure shall show a
statement of quantity

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a commodity, other than a commodity
  referred to in paragraph 46(h), that is packaged before being offered
  for sale, on the side of the package containing the commodity that is
  visible to the purchaser under normal or customary conditions of sale
  or use; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a commodity that is not packaged
  before being offered for sale, on a part of the commodity itself that
  is visible to the purchaser under normal or customary conditions of
  sale or use or on a shipping bill, bill of lading or other document
  accompanying the commodity.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/90-118, s. 14
:::

**[[47.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47.1 .sectionLabel}** The statement of
quantity for a commodity set out in column I of Schedule II.1 shall
consist of at least one statement per parameter set out in column II.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-297, s. 8
- SOR/2014-111, s. 24
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-47.1-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 47.1"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  the statement of quantity is shown on the package or on the commodity
  itself, the statement shall be shown in a manner easily legible to the
  purchaser and shall be in letters of not less than 3 mm or 1/8 inch in
  height if printed or written by hand and in letters of not less than 2
  mm or 1/12 inch in height if printed or written by any other means.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} When the statement of quantity is shown on a shipping
  bill, bill of lading or any other document accompanying the commodity,
  it shall be shown in a manner easily legible to the purchaser.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where the price of a commodity is determined on the
  basis of a unit of measurement, that unit of measurement shall be
  shown on the statement of quantity in a manner at least as prominent
  as any other unit of measurement shown on the statement of quantity.

### [Limits of Error for Commodities and Services]{.HTitleText2} {#h-533062 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/90-278, s. 3

\]
:::

- **[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the
  prescribed limits of error for the purposes of sections 9 and 33 of
  the Act are those set out in Column II of an item of the appropriate
  Part of Schedule II for the stated quantity set out in Column I of
  that item.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a commodity is weighed or measured on a device
  for which the tolerance is prescribed in section 192, 193 or 268, the
  limits of error for the commodity shall be one and one-half times the
  in-service tolerance prescribed for the device.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where the quantity of a commodity is stated in a unit
  of measurement that is not set out in column I of the applicable Part
  of Schedule II, the quantity shall be converted to the appropriate
  unit set out in column I of that Part and the limit of error set out
  in column II for that quantity shall apply.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 2, 6(F)
- SOR/98-115, s. 5
- SOR/2005-297, s. 9
:::

**[[49.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
section 34 of the Act, the limit of error applicable to the weight
declaration of a commodity used to determine rail transportation charges
for the commodity, obtained by in-motion weighing of cars carrying the
commodity is one per cent of the declared net weight.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/90-278, s. 4
:::

### [Examination]{.HTitleText2} {#h-533074 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 46

\]
:::

- **[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  an examination is made of an individually measured commodity that is
  in a liquid state and has its quantity stated in units of volume, the
  quantity of the commodity shall be determined at the temperature at
  which it is customarily measured for sale.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} When an examination is made of an individually
  measured commodity that is in a liquid state and has its quantity
  stated in units of volume at 15°C, the quantity of the commodity shall
  be determined at a temperature of 15°C.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2005-297, s. 10
- SOR/2014-111, s. 46
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-50-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 50"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  an examination is made of a standard quantity commodity that is in a
  liquid or near liquid state at a temperature of 20°C and has its
  quantity stated in units of volume, the quantity of the commodity
  shall be determined at a temperature of 20°C.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} When an examination is made of a standard quantity
  commodity that is in a frozen state and has its quantity stated in
  units of volume, the quantity of the commodity shall be determined at
  the temperature at which it is customarily offered for sale.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2005-297, s. 10
- SOR/2014-111, s. 46
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-51-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 51"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Examination by Sample]{.HTitleText2} {#h-533087 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 46

\]
:::

- **[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  examination of any quantity of prepackaged commodities, referred to as
  a lot, each unit of which purports to contain the same quantity of
  commodity, that an inspector undertakes to determine whether the lot
  meets the requirements of the Act and these Regulations respecting the
  statement of quantity, shall be made by selecting and examining a
  sample from the lot.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), where a lot contains the
  number of units set out in Column I of an item of Part I of Schedule
  III, an inspector shall select from the lot a number of units not less
  than the number set out in Column II of that item, and the number of
  units selected shall constitute the sample referred to in subsection
  (1).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where, for the purpose of determining the quantity,
  other than for establishing the weight of the package containing the
  commodity, it is necessary to destroy a certain number of units in the
  lot, an inspector shall select, for destruction, not more than 10 per
  cent of the total number of units in the lot and not less than one
  unit, and the number of units selected shall constitute the sample
  referred to in subsection (1).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The lot from which a sample was taken and examined by
  an inspector does not meet the requirements of the Act and these
  Regulations respecting the statement of quantity if the inspector
  determines that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the weighted average quantity of the units in the
    sample, as determined by the formula set out in Part II of Schedule
    III, is less than the stated quantity;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of units in the sample that contain less
    than the stated quantity by more than the prescribed limits of error
    set out in Schedule II for that quantity is equal to or greater than
    the number set out in Column II of Part IV of Schedule III for the
    sample size set out in Column I thereof; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} two or more units in the sample contain less than
    the stated quantity by more than twice the prescribed limits of
    error set out in Schedule II for that quantity.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 3, 6(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 25(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-52-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 52"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART III]{.HLabel1}[Local Standards]{.HTitleText1} {#h-533100 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-533101 .Subheading}

**[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** In this Part and in
Schedule IV,

[[cylindrical graduated standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[cylindrical graduated standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a local
    standard of volume or capacity that is made of glass or metal, has a
    cylindrical shape and, if made entirely of glass, bears graduations
    on the glass or, if made of metal, bears graduations that are
    adjacent to a glass window in the metal; ([étalon cylindrique
    gradué]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[gravimetric prover standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[gravimetric prover standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a local
    standard consisting of a tank mounted on a weighing machine and used
    for determining

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the mass of a product, or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the volume of a liquid on the basis of the mass
      registered on the weighing machine and the density of the
      liquid; ([cuve étalon gravimétrique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[hydrometer standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[hydrometer standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[ \[Repealed,
    SOR/2005-297, s. 11\]]{.Repealed}

[[master meter standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[master meter standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a local standard
    that registers by mechanical, electrical or electronic means or any
    combination thereof, in units or multiples or subdivisions of units
    of volume, the quantity of liquid that passes through the standard
    during an interval of operation; ([compteur
    étalon-témoin]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[narrow neck glass standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[narrow neck glass standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[ \[Repealed,
    SOR/93-234, s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

[[narrow neck metal standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[narrow neck metal standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[ \[Repealed,
    SOR/93-234, s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

[[narrow neck standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[narrow neck standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a local standard
    of volume or capacity that has, on or adjacent to the narrow neck of
    the standard, a mark indicating the volume or capacity of the
    standard, or that has a machined rim that indicates the volume or
    capacity of the standard; ([étalon à col étroit]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[nominal value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[nominal value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a local standard of mass or
      weight, the mass or weight shown on that standard,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a local standard of length, the
      length between any two graduations on that standard,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a narrow neck standard, the volume
      or capacity shown on that standard,

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cylindrical graduated standard,
      the maximum volume or capacity shown on that standard,

    - [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a stoppered pycnometer standard,
      the volume or capacity of the standard when filled and stoppered
      at reference conditions,

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a master meter standard,
      gravimetric prover standard, pressure hydrometer standard,
      standard of temperature or electricity measuring standard, the
      value shown by that standard for any particular test, and

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a pipe prover standard, the volume
      of the standard stabilized at a temperature of 15°C and 101.325
      kPa internal pressure as shown on the certificate issued for that
      standard pursuant to section 13 of the Act; ([valeur
      nominale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[pipe prover standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[pipe prover standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a local standard
    consisting of a pipe equipped with a piston or ball in which the
    volume of liquid displaced when the piston or ball traverses all or
    any part of the length of the pipe is registered by mechanical,
    electrical or electronic means or any combination thereof. ([tube
    étalon]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[pressure hydrometer standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[pressure hydrometer standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a local
    standard that indicates the density or relative density of a fluid
    under pressure. ([hydromètre étalon à pression]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/90-118, s. 15
- SOR/93-234, s. 2
- SOR/2005-297, s. 11
:::

### [Tolerances for Local Standards]{.HTitleText2} {#h-533124 .Subheading}

- **[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the
  amount set out in Column II of an item in the appropriate Part of
  Schedule IV is hereby prescribed to be the tolerance for the purpose
  of subsection 13(1) of the Act for a local standard that has a nominal
  value set out in Column I of that item.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the nominal value of a local standard is not set
  out in column I of the appropriate Part of Schedule IV, the tolerance
  for that standard shall be the amount resulting from linear
  interpolation between the tolerances set out in column II of that Part
  in respect of the nominal values set out in column I that are nearest
  to the nominal value of the standard.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The tolerances set out in Parts XI and XII of
  Schedule IV for the nominal value of cylindrical graduated standards
  are also the tolerances for any other values that are marked on those
  standards as intermediate graduations.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 13(1) of the Act, the
  tolerance for a local standard referred to in subsection 56(2) at any
  point within the measuring range designated by the owner of the
  standard for the purpose of use of the standard is two per cent of the
  value or reading of that point indicated by the reference standard.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/86-132, s. 1
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
- SOR/2005-297, s. 12
- SOR/2014-111, s. 26
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-54-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 54"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART IV]{.HLabel1}[Fees and Charges]{.HTitleText1} {#h-533154 .Part}

**[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/79-747,
s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

**[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,

[[travel time]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[travel time]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the time that is spent by
    an inspector travelling to and returning from the place where a
    service set out in subsection 59(1) is provided; ([temps de
    déplacement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[waiting time]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[waiting time]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the time during which an
    inspector is ready to commence or continue the provision of a
    service set out in subsection 59(1) but is prevented from so doing
    because

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a device necessary to the provision of the
      service is being altered or adjusted by another person, or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} of actions or omissions affecting the provision
      of the service which are beyond the control of the
      inspector. ([période d'attente]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"}).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-747, s. 1
- SOR/85-736, s. 2
- SOR/87-582, s. 1
- SOR/93-413, s. 1
:::

**[[58.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58.1 .sectionLabel}** The fees and charges
under this Part do not apply to services provided by an inspector who is
not employed in the federal public administration.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 28
:::

- **[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  fees and charges for the following services provided by an inspector
  shall be determined in accordance with Part I of Schedule V:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} examination of a device in accordance with a
    request referred to in section 15.1 or subsection 21(2) of the Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} examination of a device for the purposes of
    paragraph 8(b) of the Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} calibration of a standard, other than a standard
    used or intended to be used by an inspector to examine a device, at
    the request of the owner or person in possession of the standard;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} examination of a commodity at the request of the
    owner or person in possession of the commodity;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} provision of any service related to an application
    for the approval of a device or of a class, type or design of a
    device referred to in section 14; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} alteration or adjustment of a device with the
    consent of the owner or person in possession of the device.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where, in order to provide a service set out in
  subsection (1), an inspector provides or transports a piece of
  equipment or a vehicle described in column I of item 1 of Part II of
  Schedule V, the applicable charges set out in column II of that item
  shall be paid in addition to the fees and charges set out in Part I of
  that Schedule.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The charges incurred by an inspector for
  accommodation, meals, incidental expenses and transportation referred
  to in Schedule V are determined in accordance with the rates and
  allowances set out in the "Travel Directive" contained in the Treasury
  Board Manual that are in effect at the time that the service set out
  in subsection (1) is provided.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where the fees and charges set out in Schedule V are
  calculated on the basis of time, that time includes travel time and
  waiting time.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-747, s. 1
- SOR/85-736, s. 2
- SOR/87-582, s. 2
- SOR/93-413, s. 1
- SOR/2014-111, ss. 29, 49(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-59-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 59"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/94-650,
s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

**[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/85-736,
s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

**[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/93-413,
s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

**[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63 .sectionLabel}** If, in order to perform
an examination, an inspector requires any equipment, material or
commodity that is not customarily supplied by a Measurement Canada
office and that has not been supplied by the dealer or trader for whom
the examination is to be performed, the cost of purchasing or renting
the necessary equipment, material or commodity and transporting it to
and from the place where the examination is to be performed shall be
payable in addition to the fee and charge otherwise payable for the
examination.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2005-297, s. 40
- SOR/2014-111, s. 30
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-63-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 63"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Payment of Fees and Charges]{.HTitleText2} {#h-533185 .Subheading}

- **[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 21 of the Act,
  the fee and charge prescribed by these Regulations for the
  examination, altering or adjusting of a device shall be paid by the
  owner of the device or by the person in possession of the device.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a commodity is examined pursuant to the Act,
  the fee and charge prescribed by these Regulations for examining the
  commodity shall be paid by the person who requests the examination.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 46
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-64-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 64"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART V]{.HLabel1}[Specifications Relating to Devices]{.HTitleText1} {#h-533191 .Part}

### [Establishment of Specifications]{.HTitleText2} {#h-533192 .Subheading}

- **[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), this Part
  establishes the specifications relating to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the design, composition, construction and
    performance to which a class, type or design of device described in
    this Part shall conform before it may be approved for use in trade
    pursuant to section 3 of the Act; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the installation and use of any class, type or
    design of device described in this Part.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} This Part does not apply to devices to which the
  Specifications Relating to Non-automatic Weighing Devices (1998) or
  the Specifications Relating to Timber Dimension Measuring Devices
  apply.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/94-691, s. 4(F)
- SOR/98-115, s. 6
- SOR/2005-130, s. 6
- [SOR/2024-183, s. 4](#1488109-1488664){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1488109-1488664.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-65-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 65"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION I]{.HLabel2}[Devices in General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-533200 .Subheading}

#### [Design, Composition and Construction]{.HTitleText3} {#h-533201 .Subheading}

**[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66 .sectionLabel}** Every device for use in
trade shall be of such design, composition and construction as will
reasonably ensure its ability to provide satisfactory accuracy
throughout its normal life and eliminate means of perpetrating fraud
when in use.

#### [Performance]{.HTitleText3} {#h-533203 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2005-297, s, 42(F)

\]
:::

- **[[67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  device for use in trade set out in Column I of an item of the table to
  this subsection shall be of such stability that under all temperatures
  and other conditions that may normally be encountered in use, and
  without adjustments other than those specifically provided for use of
  the device, the device is capable of measuring accurately within
  applicable in-service limits of error throughout the applicable period
  set out in Column II of that item.

  <figure id="533208">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom"></th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column I</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column II</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Device</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Period</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Measuring tank having a
  capacity greater than 450 L or 100 gallons <img src="/img/ii_spacer.gif"
  class="LeaderRightJustified-dot" alt="blank line" /></td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom bottom">5 years</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Slow-flow meter having a
  flow rate of 115 L or less per hour or 25 gallons or less per hour <img
  src="/img/ii_spacer.gif" class="LeaderRightJustified-dot"
  alt="blank line" /></td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom bottom">5 years</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight top">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight top">Any device not described in item 1 or 2
  <img src="/img/ii_spacer.gif" class="LeaderRightJustified-dot"
  alt="blank line" /></td>
  <td class="bottom">2 years</td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a device is tested for performance and a
  specific value for the known test quantity is not set out in the
  applicable table to this Part or in specifications established
  pursuant to subsection 13(1), the acceptance limits of error and
  in-service limits of error shall be determined by linear interpolation
  between the lower and higher limits of error nearest to the known test
  quantity.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/80-290, s. 2
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
- SOR/90-118, s. 17
- SOR/2005-297, s. 14
- SOR/2014-111, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-67-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 67"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Installation and Use]{.HTitleText3} {#h-533217 .Subheading}

- **[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  device for use in trade shall be installed, maintained and used so as
  to permit accurate measurement and eliminate means of perpetrating
  fraud when in use.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A device referred to in subsection (1) shall not be
  used to measure any quantity of commodity unless the limit of error
  set out in Column II of an item of the appropriate Part of Schedule II
  for the stated quantity set out in Column I of that item is equal to
  or greater than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the value of the minimum increment of registration
    of the device if the device has digital means of registration; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} one-half the value of the minimum increment of
    registration of the device if the device has analogue means of
    registration only.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/90-118, s. 18
:::

**[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69 .sectionLabel}** The installation and use
of every device for use in trade shall conform to instructions for
installation and use issued by the manufacturer or importer, unless
there is an apparent contradiction between such instructions and the
specifications set out in this Part or established by the Minister, in
which case the specifications set out in this Part or established by the
Minister shall prevail.

**[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70 .sectionLabel}** If the use of a device in
any manner or for any purpose is prohibited in either the certificate
issued at the time of the most recent examination performed under the
Act or in the notice of approval of the device issued under section 3 of
the Act or under a previous enactment, the device shall, throughout the
period of its use in trade, have closely associated with it or
prominently displayed on it a readily legible sign or notice setting out
the manner or purpose of use that is prohibited.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 31
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-70-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 70"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION II]{.HLabel2}[Weights]{.HTitleText2} {#h-533229 .Subheading}

#### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-533230 .Subheading}

**[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71 .sectionLabel}** In this Division,
[[weight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a weight for use in trade.

#### [Design, Composition and Construction]{.HTitleText3} {#h-533232 .Subheading}

**[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** Weights shall be composed
of any metal or metals not softer than brass, except that weights of
less than one gram in the metric system or less than 10 grains in the
Canadian system shall be composed of corrosion resistant metal.

**[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** Weight reducing holes
shall be so made as to prevent the retaining of adjusting lead or
foreign matter and the entire surface of weights shall be smooth and
reasonably free from grooves or indentations in which foreign matter
could accumulate.

**[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74 .sectionLabel}** If a weight is equipped
with a ring, the ring shall not be removable.

**[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75 .sectionLabel}** Cast iron weights shall
be painted with at least one coat of suitable waterproof metal sealer or
primer, and may be also painted with a finish coat if the resulting
aggregate coat is thin and durable.

**[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76 .sectionLabel}** When a cast iron weight
is painted, it shall be calibrated after it receives the final paint
coat.

**[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** All adjusting holes
intended to hold lead in a weight shall be undercut or so made as to
ensure that the lead is securely held in place and in no case shall the
lead project beyond the surface of the weight.

**[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78 .sectionLabel}** At the time of an
examination for the purposes of paragraph 8(b) or 26(1)(c) of the Act,
not more than two adjusting holes shall be provided in a single weight
and at every examination there shall be lead in each adjusting hole
sufficient to accept an examination mark.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 32
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-78-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 78"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79 .sectionLabel}** A counterpoise weight the
principal section of which is too thin to hold an adjusting hole shall
have a boss or lug of a size sufficient to hold the adjusting hole.

**[[80]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80 .sectionLabel}** A weight with a cavity
for loose adjusting material that is fitted with a screw top or plug
shall be provided with a positive means of locking, other than by
friction, the top or plug to the body of the weight.

**[[81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81 .sectionLabel}** Weights shall be
constructed to represent

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} multiples or submultiples of the kilogram, gram or
  milligram having values equal to 1, 2 or 5 × 10^n^ units, where "n"
  represents a whole number, whether positive, negative or equal to
  zero;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} multiples or decimal submultiples of the pound or
  troy ounce;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} multiples or binary submultiples of the pound or
  ounce; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} multiples or decimal submultiples of the grain.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-17, s. 1
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-81-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 81"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Performance]{.HTitleText3} {#h-533251 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2005-297, s. 42(F)

\]
:::

**[[82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-82 .sectionLabel}** When a weight that has a
marked weight set out in column I of an item in a table to sections 84
to 88 is tested for acceptance limits of error in relation to a local
standard weight, the weight is considered to be within the acceptance
limits of error if its actual weight determined by the test

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is not less than its marked weight; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} does not exceed its marked weight by an amount in
  excess of the amount set out in Column II of that item.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
- SOR/2012-28, s. 1
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-82-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 82"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-83 .sectionLabel}** When a weight that has a
marked weight set out in column I of an item in a table to sections 84
to 88 is tested for in-service limits of error in relation to a local
standard weight, the weight is considered to be within the in-service
limits of error if its actual weight determined by the test

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is not more than its marked weight by an amount in
  excess of the amount set out in Column III of that item; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not less than its marked weight by an amount in
  excess of the amount set out in Column IV of that item.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
- SOR/2012-28, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-83-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 83"){.PITLink}
:::

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The limits of error for weights for use in
  conjunction with equal-arm pan-over-beam scales are twice the limits
  of error set out in the table to subsection (1).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The limits of error for weights for use as ratio
  weights where the ratio is in excess of 100:1 are one-half the limits
  of error set out in the table to subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
:::

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The limits of error for weights for use in
  conjunction with equal-arm pan-over-beam scales are twice the limits
  of error set out in the table to subsection (1).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The limits of error for weights for use as ratio
  weights where the ratio is in excess of 100:1 are one-half the limits
  of error set out in the table to subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
:::

#### [Use of Weights]{.HTitleText3} {#h-533315 .Subheading}

**[[89]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-89 .sectionLabel}** A weight shall be used
only with a weighing machine with which it is compatible.

**[[90]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90 .sectionLabel}** When not in use, a weight
that, due to its small size, is not individually marked with an
examination mark shall be stored in a container that bears the
examination mark.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 33
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-90-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 90"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION III]{.HLabel2}[Static Volumetric Measures]{.HTitleText2} {#h-533320 .Subheading}

#### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-533321 .Subheading}

**[[91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91 .sectionLabel}** In this Division,
[[measure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a static volumetric measure for
use in trade.

#### [Design, Composition and Construction]{.HTitleText3} {#h-533323 .Subheading}

**[[92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92 .sectionLabel}** Measures shall be free
from ridges or indentations likely to entrap dirt or the commodity
measured and shall empty completely when inverted.

**[[93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93 .sectionLabel}** The internal horizontal
dimensions of a measure for liquids shall be such that the removal from
it of an amount equal to the limit of error in deficiency will lower the
liquid level by not less than 2.4 mm or 3/32 inch or, if the measure is
transparent, by not less than 1.2 mm or 3/64 inch.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
:::

**[[94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 95, a
measure that is not transparent shall be so designed that its capacity
is established by the brim and when a measure is equipped with a spout,
pouring lip or anti-splash protection, not less than one third of the
brim of the measure shall be in a plane parallel to the base.

**[[95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-95 .sectionLabel}** When a measure is
equipped with a lid or cover, the capacity of the measure shall be
determined by the bottom of the neck unless a permanent defining line or
indicator is provided on the neck.

**[[96]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96 .sectionLabel}** Unless otherwise
authorized by the Minister, all measures for solids shall be
cylindrical, designed to be struck with a roller and be of a diameter
that does not differ from either the depth or double the depth by more
than five per cent.

**[[97]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-97 .sectionLabel}** A measure may be composed
of any suitable material that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} has sufficient strength, rigidity and durability to
  maintain its form and accuracy and resist indentation, distortion or
  breakage under ordinary conditions of trade use; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is impervious to the commodity for which it is
  intended.

**[[98]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-98 .sectionLabel}** Unless clearly and
permanently marked with the words "Calibrated to Contain" ("calibré pour
recevoir"), a measure for liquids shall be of such capacity that, when
filled to capacity with water, the indicated volume can be poured out of
the measure.

#### [Performance]{.HTitleText3} {#h-533335 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2005-297, s. 42(F)

\]
:::

**[[99]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-99 .sectionLabel}** When a measure that has
an indicated volume set out in Column I of an item in a table to
sections 101 to 103 is tested for acceptance limits of error in relation
to a relevant local standard, the measure is within the acceptance
limits of error if its actual volume determined by the test

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is not less than its indicated volume; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} does not exceed its indicated volume by an amount in
  excess of the amount set out in Column II of that item.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
- SOR/2012-28, s. 13(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-99-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 99"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[100]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-100 .sectionLabel}** When a measure that has
an indicated volume set out in Column I of an item in a table to
sections 101 to 103 is tested for in-service limits of error in relation
to a relevant local standard, the measure is within the in-service
limits of error if its actual volume determined by the test

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} does not exceed its indicated volume by an amount in
  excess of the amount set out in Column III of that item; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not less than its indicated volume by an amount in
  excess of the amount set out in Column IV of that item.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
:::

- **[[101]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), the
  limits of error set out in the following table apply to all measures
  used for measuring liquids or solids in metric units of volume or
  capacity:

  headers="t49h1 t49h8 t49h63 t49h3 t49h6 t49h11 t49h16">5</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight top" headers="t49h7">8</td>
  <td id="t49h68" class="noBorderRight top"
  headers="t49h1 t49h8 t49h58">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight top"
  headers="t49h1 t49h8 t49h68 t49h2 t49h4 t49h9 t49h14">15</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight top"
  headers="t49h1 t49h8 t49h68 t49h3 t49h5 t49h10 t49h15">15</td>
  <td class="top"
  headers="t49h1 t49h8 t49h68 t49h3 t49h6 t49h11 t49h16">7½</td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The limits of error in the table to subsection (1)
  apply to tests made on a "to deliver" basis with water at room
  temperature.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The limits of error for measures used with grain
  testing scales are ¼ the limits of error set out in the table to
  subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
:::

#### [Use of Measures]{.HTitleText3} {#h-533383 .Subheading}

**[[104]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104 .sectionLabel}** A measure shall rest on
a level surface when the correctness of filling is being determined by
the user.

**[[105]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-105 .sectionLabel}** Measures shall be used
in a manner that will prevent dents or other damage thereto that would
cause a change in capacity.

**[[106]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-106 .sectionLabel}** Measures for liquids
shall be permitted to drain for sufficient time to ensure that the
liquid delivered is not less than the indicated volume by more than the
limits of error set out in Column IV of the tables to sections 101 to
103.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
:::
