  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Known Test
  Quantity</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Acceptance
  Limits of Error</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">In-Service
  Limits of Error</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">Any volume above
  the minimum volume set out in specifications established by the Minister
  for slow-flow meters or for a design of slow-flow meter (during tests
  made at 0.5 litres per hour or 0.10 gallons per hour)</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3% of known test quantity</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom">4% of known test quantity</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight" scope="row">Any volume above the minimum
  volume set out in specifications established by the Minister for
  slow-flow meters or for a design of slow-flow meter (during tests made
  at 3.4 litres per hour or 0.75 gallons per hour)</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">½% of known test quantity</td>
  <td>¾% of known test quantity</td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The limits of error set out in the tables to
  subsections (2) and (3) refer to the results of bench tests made in
  accordance with specifications established by the Minister for
  slow-flow meters.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
:::

- **[[270]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-270 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section,

  [[API Gravity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[API Gravity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the amount obtained
      using the following formula:

      ::: {#534070 .Definition}
      API Gravity = 141.5 ÷ (relative density 60/60°F) -- 131.5;
      :::

      ([densité API]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[API Standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[API Standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Manual of Petroleum
      Measurement Standards, published by the American Petroleum
      Institute in August, 1980, with the designation "API, Standard
      2540"; ([norme API]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ASTM]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ASTM]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the American Society for
      Testing and Materials; ([ASTM]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ASTM-IP]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ASTM-IP]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the American Society for
      Testing and Materials --- Institute of
      Petroleum; ([ASTM-IP]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[VCF]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[VCF]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a mechanical or
      electronic automatic temperature compensator, the applicable
      volume correction factor

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the temperature of the measured liquid
        during each test of the compensator, in the column corresponding
        to the density at 15°C in kilograms per cubic metre for which
        the compensator is marked or adjusted, or to the density
        corresponding to the cubical coefficient of thermal expansion,
        relative density or API gravity for which the compensator is
        marked or adjusted, that is set out

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of natural crude oil, in Table
          54A of the API Standard,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of generalized liquid petroleum
          products, in Table 54B of the API Standard, and

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of lubricating oils, in Table
          54D of the API Standard,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the temperature of the measured liquid
        during each test of the compensator, in the column corresponding
        to the agreed or experimentally determined cubical coefficient
        of thermal expansion at 15°C for that liquid and for which the
        compensator is marked or adjusted, that is set out, in the case
        of special petroleum liquids to which Tables 54A, 54B and 54D of
        the API Standard are not applicable, in Table 54C of the API
        Standard, or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} at the temperature of the measured liquid
        during each test of the compensator, in the column corresponding
        to the density at 15°C in grams per litre for which the
        compensator is marked or adjusted, or to the density
        corresponding to the cubical coefficient of thermal expansion,
        relative density or API gravity for which the compensator is
        marked or adjusted, that is set out, in the case of liquefied
        petroleum gases, natural gas liquids and asphalts, in Table 54
        of the ASTM-IP Petroleum Measurement Tables, with designation
        ASTM D1250, 1953 Metric Edition. ([FCV]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (4) to (6), the limits of
  error set out in the table to this subsection apply in respect of
  mechanical automatic temperature compensators used in conjunction with
  meters in the measurement of liquids.

  <figure id="534084">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom"></th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom" scope="col">Column
  I</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom" scope="col">Column
  II</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom bottom" scope="col">Column III</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Known Test
  Quantity</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Acceptance
  Limits of Error</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">In-Service
  Limits of Error</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight top">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight top" scope="row">Test volume shall be in
  accordance with specifications established pursuant to section 13 for
  mechanical automatic temperature compensators for use with any device or
  class, type or design of device</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight top">The ratio NET/GROSS shall lie in the range
  from
  <p>(VCF-0.0010</p>
  <p>-0.00018ΔT)</p>
  <p>to</p>
  <p>(VCF+0.0010</p>
  <p>+0.00009ΔT)</p></td>
  <td class="top">The ratio NET/GROSS shall lie in the range from
  <p>(VCF-0.0020</p>
  <p>-0.00036ΔT)</p>
  <p>to</p>
  <p>(VCF+0.0020</p>
  <p>+0.00018ΔT)</p></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the table to subsection (2) and in subsections (5)
  and (7),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [[NET]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} is the corrected
    registration of the register and [[GROSS]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} is
    the uncorrected registration of the register; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [[ΔT]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} is the difference in
    degrees Celsius

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} between the test temperature and 15°C where the
      compensator is used with a meter designed to measure liquids at
      ambient temperature, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} between the test temperature and the mid-range
      temperature of the compensator, where the compensator is used with
      a meter designed to measure liquids at temperatures other than
      ambient temperature, and for the purposes of calculating the
      limits of error, "ΔT" shall not exceed 15°C except in the case of
      a compensator used with a meter designed to measure liquids at
      ambient temperature.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The acceptance limits of error set out in Column II
  of the table to subsection (2) apply only to tests made under
  controlled conditions, and for tests made under other conditions the
  limits of error set out in Column III of that table apply.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} In addition to being within applicable limits of
  error, the NET/GROSS ratios shall agree within 0.001 on at least two
  of three consecutive tests at the same temperature under controlled
  conditions.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The limits of error for mechanical automatic
  temperature compensators submitted for approval pursuant to section 3
  of the Act shall be one half the limits of error set out in the table
  to subsection (2).

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} The limits of error set out in the table to this
  subsection apply in respect of electronic automatic temperature
  compensators used in conjunction with meters in the measurement of
  liquids.

  <figure id="534099">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom"></th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom" scope="col">Column
  I</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom" scope="col">Column
  II</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom bottom" scope="col">Column III</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Known Test
  Quantity</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Acceptance

  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">In-Service

  </tr>

  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight" scope="row">Test volume shall be in accordance
  with specifications established pursuant to section 13 for electronic
  automatic temperature compensators for use with any device or class,
  type or design of device</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">The VCF applied to the gross registration
  shall be not more than the VCF corresponding to a temperature that is
  0.5°C less than the test temperature, and not less than the VCF
  corresponding to a temperature that is 0.5°C more than the test
  temperature</td>
  <td>The VCF applied to the gross registration shall be not more than the
  VCF corresponding to a temperature that is 1°C less than the test
  temperature, and not less than the VCF corresponding to a temperature
  that is 1°C more than the test temperature</td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} Where an automatic temperature compensator referred
  to in subsection (7) is not marked as described in the definition
  [[VCF]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection (1), the printed ticket,
  card, bill of lading or other printed record of a transaction shall
  specify the density, relative density, API gravity or cubical
  coefficient of thermal expansion, as appropriate, for which the
  compensator is adjusted, for each delivery by the meter.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
- SOR/90-118, s. 37
- SOR/2005-297, s. 20
:::

#### [Installation and Use]{.HTitleText3} {#h-534108 .Subheading}

**[[271]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-271 .sectionLabel}** A meter shall be
properly suited to the particular installation in which it is to be
used, with respect to pressure, rate of flow, temperature, compatibility
with the characteristics of the liquid to be measured and method of use
so that, for all normally occurring variations in operating conditions,
measurement by the meter remains within the applicable limits of error.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
:::

**[[272]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-272 .sectionLabel}** The equipment and
accessories installed with a meter shall be selected and installed so as
to minimize their interference with the proper operation and accuracy of
measurement of the meter.

**[[273]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-273 .sectionLabel}** The pump suction piping
installed with a meter shall be as short and unencumbered as possible
and all liquid supply systems associated with a meter shall be arranged
to minimize the ingress of air, water or other foreign matter, including
any liquid other than the one the meter is intended to measure.

**[[274]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-274 .sectionLabel}** A meter and all
equipment and accessories associated with it in an installation shall be
installed so as to minimize the evolution of dissolved air and gases and
to minimize the vaporization of the liquid prior to or during
measurement or in the delivery conduit.

- **[[275]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-275 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  meter shall be installed so that it can only be operated when the
  liquid in the meter is at or above atmospheric pressure, and in any
  installation where the liquid pressure could fall below atmospheric
  pressure, a suitable vacuum breaker shall be installed at the outlet
  of the meter.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A meter shall be operated only when the liquid in the
  meter is at or above atmospheric pressure.

**[[276]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-276 .sectionLabel}** When a meter is
installed in a measuring system, the system shall be equipped with
effective automatic means to minimize the passage of air or vapour
through the meter.

**[[277]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-277 .sectionLabel}** A meter shall have
installed immediately upstream therefrom a screen, strainer, filter,
integral filter or such other approved means as will prevent foreign
matter from passing through the meter.

**[[278]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-278 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
sections 276 and 277, a close-coupled strainer and automatic air release
valve is sufficient in meter installations where only free air or vapour
is to be removed from the liquid.

**[[279]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-279 .sectionLabel}** The air or vapour
eliminator installed with a meter shall minimize the passage of air or
vapour through the measuring element of the meter when the supply of
liquid is exhausted or appropriate means shall be provided to
automatically stop the flow of liquid or to indicate to the person using
the meter that air or vapour is passing through the meter.

**[[280]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-280 .sectionLabel}** Sealed flow control
valves or other means shall be installed with a meter to limit the flow
through the meter to its rated maximum flow.

**[[281]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-281 .sectionLabel}** Unless a meter is
equipped with a reversing register and totalizer in accordance with
section 250, it shall be installed so as to minimize any reverse flow
through it or the piping or equipment upstream or downstream from it
that could affect the accuracy of its measurement.

- **[[282]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-282 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[transfer point]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the fixed
  point in a measuring assembly where transfer of possession of a
  quantity of liquid contained in the measuring assembly takes place.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A meter shall be installed so that the piping between
  the meter and the transfer point remains full of liquid during and
  after a transaction unless the liquid, because of its nature, cannot
  remain in the meter, and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a meter installed to deliver a
    quantity of liquid, so that the piping downstream of the transfer
    point shall empty at the completion of the transaction; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a meter installed to receive a
    quantity of liquid, so that the piping upstream of the transfer
    point shall empty at the completion of the transaction.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 289 and except as otherwise
  allowed by any additional specifications established pursuant to
  section 27, a meter shall be installed

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} so that all piping downstream of the meter, where
    the meter is installed to deliver a quantity of liquid, or all
    piping upstream of the meter, where the meter is installed to
    receive a quantity of liquid, may be readily examined; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with only one delivery outlet unless the meter is
    installed

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} so that diversion of flow is readily apparent to
      the purchaser or the purchaser's agent or mandatary by automatic
      means such as visible valves or lights with explanatory signs that
      indicate which outlets are in operation,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to fuel or defuel aircraft, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} to be used exclusively to load vehicle-mounted
      propane tanks with a capacity greater than 5 000 L.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/90-118, s. 38
- SOR/2014-111, ss. 35(E), 46
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-282-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 282"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[283]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-283 .sectionLabel}** A meter, other than a
slow-flow meter, slow-flow dispenser, dispenser of the service station
type for motor fuel or a meter equipped with a preset valve, shall have
a quick-acting valve installed near its outlet for examination purposes.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 46
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-283-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 283"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[284]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-284 .sectionLabel}** A meter, other than a
slow-flow meter, shall be installed so that it has a convenient means
for delivering the measured product when the meter installation is
examined.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 36
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-284-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 284"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[285]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-285 .sectionLabel}** Any automatic valve
that can have a throttling effect on the flow of liquid shall be
installed downstream from the meter.

- **[[286]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-286
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise authorized by
  specifications established by the Minister, the outlet end of the
  delivery hose of a pump-supplied meter shall be equipped with a
  spring-loaded anti-drain valve or its equivalent.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a meter with
  collapsible hoses used on aircraft refuellers or to a meter designed
  for defuelling aircraft.

**[[287]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-287 .sectionLabel}** The discharge hose
installed with a meter supplied by gravity head may be of the dry hose
type without a shut-off valve at the discharge end, but it shall be of
such length, size and stiffness as to ensure complete drainage and there
shall be means provided in the meter system to show the empty condition
of the hose and to prevent the meter from draining.

**[[288]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-288 .sectionLabel}** Where it is necessary

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to limit changes in the viscosity of, or the
  solubility of material dissolved in, the liquid to be measured by the
  meter, or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} to limit the rate of change of the temperature or
  pressure of the liquid to be measured by the meter when the meter is
  equipped with an automatic compensator,

appropriate automatic controls shall be installed in the meter system to
permit the meter to measure accurately.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/90-118, s. 39
:::

- **[[289]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-289 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  piping on the downstream side of meters may be concealed and the
  outlets interconnected where the meters

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} are used

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} at pipeline terminals, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} exclusively for loading or unloading railway
      tank cars or loading or unloading tanker ships; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} have adequately interlocked manual or automatic
    valves with means for leakage detection incorporated in the meter
    systems to prevent the diversion of measured liquid.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The piping on the downstream side of the meters may
  be concealed where the meters have more than one outlet and are used
  exclusively for refuelling trucks.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-297, s. 21
:::

**[[290]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-290 .sectionLabel}** A meter shall be
operated only at flow rates between the rated minimum and maximum flow
rates marked on the name plate of the meter.

**[[291]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-291 .sectionLabel}** If any part of a meter
system is drained for any reason the entire system shall be re-primed
and re-filled and the register set to zero before the meter is used for
the next measurement.

**[[292]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-292 .sectionLabel}** A flow transmitter for
input to an electronic data processing system may be attached to a
liquid meter and the output from the data processing system used for
invoicing purposes if the purchaser of the liquid being measured or
their agent or mandatary is given a printed copy of the data recorded by
the data processing system before they leave the trader's premises.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 37
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-292-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 292"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[293]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-293 .sectionLabel}** A flow transmitter may
be installed at a key-operated self-service meter installation to drive
the auxiliary accumulative totalizers for individual customers if the
purchaser of the liquid being measured is able to visually check the
advance of their totalizer against the indication of the primary
indicator at the time of delivery of the liquid.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 37
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-293-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 293"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[294]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-294 .sectionLabel}** Where a meter mounted
on a vehicle tank is used in circumstances where the purchaser does not
normally view the meter during delivery of a liquid, the meter shall be
equipped with an interlocked ticket printer that shall be used for each
delivery of the liquid.

**[[295]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-295 .sectionLabel}** Tickets used with a
meter equipped with an automatic temperature or other compensator shall
bear statements that the volume indicated thereon has been adjusted to
correspond to an equivalent volume at a standard condition stated on the
ticket.

### [DIVISION XII]{.HLabel2}[Measuring and Vehicle Tanks]{.HTitleText2} {#h-534173 .Subheading}

**[[296]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-296 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2014-111, s. 38\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-296-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 296"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Design, Composition and Construction]{.HTitleText3} {#h-534175 .Subheading}

**[[297]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-297 .sectionLabel}** A measuring tank and
its piping system and any equipment or accessories that can affect its
accuracy of measurement shall be of such design, composition and
construction that when a delivery is made from any capacity indication,
the quantity of liquid delivered through any discharge valve rigidly
connected to the measuring tank is within the prescribed limits of error
for the measuring tank.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-297-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 297"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[298]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-298 .sectionLabel}** The shells or bulkheads
of a measuring tank shall consist of such material and be so constructed
that they will not become distorted under any condition of loading under
normal conditions of use.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-298-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 298"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[299]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-299 .sectionLabel}** A measuring tank shall
have an effective means of automatic venting to permit the free passage
of air into and out of the measuring tank when it is emptied and filled.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-299-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 299"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[300]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-300 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 320,
there shall be no pipe or other flow connection between two or more
measuring tanks that are separately calibrated, but a battery of
calibrated measuring tanks may be connected to a common discharge
manifold that prevents flow from one measuring tank to another.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-300-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 300"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[301]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-301 .sectionLabel}** The capacity indicators
on a measuring tank shall consist of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} graduations on or adjacent to a gauge glass or tube;
  or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} appropriate internal capacity indicators.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-234, s. 2
- SOR/2014-111, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-301-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 301"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[302]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-302 .sectionLabel}** A capacity indicator
shall only be installed in a measuring tank at any level where a
quantity of liquid equal to the limit of error referred to in section
325 for the indicated quantity causes the liquid at that level to rise
by at least 2.5 mm or 3/32 of an inch.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
- SOR/90-118, s. 41
- SOR/2014-111, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-302-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 302"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[303]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-303 .sectionLabel}** A maximum capacity
indicator shall be positioned to allow a volume equal to at least one
per cent of the capacity of the measuring tank for the thermal expansion
of the liquid.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-303-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 303"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[304]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-304 .sectionLabel}** When an internal
capacity indicator is installed in a measuring tank,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the indicator shall be attached to a permanent
  section of the measuring tank; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a means shall be provided to attach a seal on the
  indicator.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-234, s. 2
- SOR/94-691, s. 4
- SOR/2014-111, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-304-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 304"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[305]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-305 .sectionLabel}** A measuring tank
equipped with an internal capacity indicator shall have a suitable
opening for the convenient installation, adjustment and sealing of the
indicator assembly and the capacity indicator shall be located so that
it can be seen under normal conditions of use.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-305-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 305"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[306]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-306 .sectionLabel}** Gauge glass fittings
shall not, except on measuring tanks for liquefied gases, be of the ball
check type or be of any type designed to prevent loss of liquid in case
of glass breakage, but a bottom gauge glass fitting may include an
emergency valve if means are provided thereon to allow an inspector to
seal the valve in the open position.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-306-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 306"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[307]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-307 .sectionLabel}** The discharge hose on a
measuring tank that discharges by gravity shall normally be of the dry
hose type, without a shut-off valve at its discharge end, shall be of
such length, size and stiffness as will ensure its complete drainage and
shall be provided with a means to indicate when all of the measured
liquid has been delivered thereby.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-307-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 307"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[308]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-308 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  measuring tank that discharges through a pump shall have an automatic
  means of ensuring that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of liquid in the pump suction and
    discharge lines remains constant; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all the measured liquid is delivered through the
    pump.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a measuring tank
  used for bunker oils and similar liquids.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-308-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 308"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[309]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-309 .sectionLabel}** A fixed measuring tank
shall have suitably installed and identified levelling ledges that will
permit the detection of any tilting of the measuring tank from the
position in which it was calibrated.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-309-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 309"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[310]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-310 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  portable measuring tank shall be equipped with permanent level gauges
  to enable it to be levelled for examination and use.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A portable measuring tank with a gauge glass shall be
  equipped with levelling jacks to enable it to be levelled for
  examination and use.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-234, s. 2(F)
- SOR/2014-111, s. 39
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-310-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 310"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[311]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-311 .sectionLabel}** A vehicle tank shall be
designed so as to permit the complete discharge of the contents of the
tank and piping when the vehicle is on a surface that is within three
degrees of level or shall be equipped with clearly defined and
appropriately located means to indicate when an off-level position will
prevent the tank and the piping from filling or draining properly.

**[[312]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-312 .sectionLabel}** An internal capacity
indicator on a vehicle tank shall be centrally located in the tank or
compartment, affixed to a mounting bracket on the dome neck by brazing,
welding or bolts and shall be sealable.

**[[313]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-313 .sectionLabel}** When two or more
capacity indicators on a vehicle tank are affixed to a mounting bracket,
the bracket shall be held in a vertical position by a suitable brace or
other reinforcement.

**[[314]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-314 .sectionLabel}** Internal capacity
indicators on a vehicle tank shall be adjustable in increments of 0.8 mm
or 1/32 inch or less.

**[[315]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315 .sectionLabel}** When a vehicle tank is
composed of two or more separate measuring compartments, any space
between the compartments shall be equipped with drains capable of
indicating leakage into the space.

**[[316]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-316 .sectionLabel}** The outlet piping from
a vehicle tank may be split into a bottom-fill connection and a drain
connection, but where the bottom loading stub can trap air in excess of
0.125 per cent of the capacity of the tank to the lowest marker when the
tank is filled from the top, the tank shall be calibrated for top-fill.

- **[[317]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-317
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a common
  manifold connected to a vehicle tank may be split into two sections
  separated by means of a valve if the valve is designed so that it
  cannot be left in any open position.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A gate valve may be used in place of the valve
  referred to in subsection (1) if the volume that can be diverted by
  leaving the gate valve open does not exceed 0.125 per cent of the
  capacity of the smallest tank connected to the manifold.

**[[318]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-318 .sectionLabel}** [\[Revoked, SOR/93-234,
s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

**[[319]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-319 .sectionLabel}** The piping on a vehicle
tank restricted for use with bunker oil may be concealed by insulation
or by parts of the tank to prevent the bunker oil from congealing.

**[[320]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-320 .sectionLabel}** Individual compartments
on a vehicle tank restricted for use with bunker oil may be connected to
a common discharge manifold without any automatic means of preventing
flow between the compartments.

#### [Performance]{.HTitleText3} {#h-534240 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2005-297, s. 42(F)

\]
:::

**[[321]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-321 .sectionLabel}** A measuring tank shall
be tested and calibrated with a liquid that has a thermal expansion
coefficient, volatility and viscosity not greater than those of domestic
furnace fuel oil and that will not have a corrosive effect on the tank.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 40
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-321-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 321"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[322]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-322 .sectionLabel}** A measuring tank shall
be tested and calibrated when all necessary supports have been installed
and it is resting in the position in which it is designed to be used.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 40
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-322-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 322"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[323]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-323 .sectionLabel}** A measuring tank, other
than a vehicle tank, shall be calibrated to a valve immediately adjacent
to the outlet connection on the tank.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 40
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-323-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 323"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[324]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-324 .sectionLabel}** Unless otherwise
authorized by specifications established by the Minister, a measuring
tank that has a gauge glass or a slip type gauge shall not be calibrated
for a delivery of less than 50 per cent of the measuring tank capacity.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-324-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 324"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[325]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-325
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), when a
  measuring tank is tested for acceptance limits of error using a known
  test volume determined in relation to a relevant local standard and
  set out in Column I of an item of a table to subsection (3) or (4),
  the measuring tank is within the acceptance limits of error in respect
  of that known test volume if the actual volume of the measuring tank

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} does not exceed the test volume by an amount in
    excess of the amount set out in Column II of that item; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not less than the test volume by an amount in
    excess of the amount set out in Column II of that item.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), when a measuring tank is
  tested for in-service limits of error using a known test volume
  determined in relation to a relevant local standard and set out in
  Column I of an item of a table to subsection (3) or (4), the measuring
  tank is within the in-service limits of error in respect of that known
  test volume if the actual volume of the measuring tank

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} does not exceed the test volume by an amount in
    excess of the amount set out in Column III of that item; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not less than the test volume by an amount in
    excess of the amount set out in Column III of that item.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), the limits of error set
  out in the table to this subsection apply to all measuring tanks that
  are calibrated in terms of metric units of volume.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The limits of error for a measuring tank that is
  designed for use in measuring liquefied gases are twice the applicable
  limits of error set out in the table to subsection (3) or (4).

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The limits of error set out in the tables to
  subsections (3) and (4) refer to the total inaccuracy of measurement
  by the measuring tank, including errors due to entrapment of air or
  liquid by parts of the measuring tank or its associated piping,
  equipment or accessories during filling or emptying.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/89-570, s. 6(F)
- SOR/94-691, s. 4(E)
- SOR/2014-111, ss. 41, 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-325-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 325"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[326]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-326 .sectionLabel}** A vehicle tank shall be
tested to ensure that air or liquid is not trapped therein in excess of
0.125 per cent of the capacity of the tank under conditions of normal
use.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 47(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-326-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 326"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Installation and Use]{.HTitleText3} {#h-534283 .Subheading}

**[[327]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-327 .sectionLabel}** A fixed measuring tank
shall be installed on substantial supports adequate to ensure that the
position of the measuring tank remains constant and level with reference
to the levelling ledges.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-327-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 327"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[328]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-328 .sectionLabel}** A fixed measuring tank
shall be level when filled, as shown by the levels installed on the tank
or by the levelling ledges, and, when in use, a portable measuring tank
or vehicle tank shall be filled and emptied when it is as close to level
conditions as possible.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 42
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-328-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 328"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[329]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-329 .sectionLabel}** When a measuring tank
is being filled, care shall be taken to minimize the possibility of air
being trapped in the piping or entrained in the liquid.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-329-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 329"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[330]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-330 .sectionLabel}** A measuring tank shall
not be used for deliveries of quantities between internal capacity
indicators.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-111, s. 45
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-330-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 330"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[331]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-331 .sectionLabel}** A vehicle tank not
equipped with level gauges shall, when being used to measure, be on a
surface that is within three degrees of level.

**[[332]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-332 .sectionLabel}** A vehicle tank that is
equipped with level gauges shall, when being used to measure, be level
within the limits marked on the level gauges.

**[[333]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-333 .sectionLabel}** A vehicle tank shall be
supported in a manner that will not distort the tank when the vehicle is
driven under conditions of normal use.

**[[334]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-334 .sectionLabel}** If emergency valves are
installed on a vehicle tank calibrated with wet lines, they shall be
open when the tank is being filled.

## [PART VI]{.HLabel1}[Authorization of Use of Units of Measurement]{.HTitleText1} {#h-534300 .Part}

measurement described in Column I of an item in the following table and
defined in Column II of that item may be used for the particular purpose
described in Column III of that item:

## [PART VII]{.HLabel1}[Metric Conversion]{.HTitleText1} {#h-534310 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/79-390, s. 1

\]
:::

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Retailing of Gasoline and Diesel Fuel]{.HTitleText2} {#h-534313 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/79-390, s. 1

\]
:::

- **[[336]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-336 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this Division, [[meter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a volumetric liquid
  measuring machine for use in the retail trade of gasoline or diesel
  fuel.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3), (4), (5) and (7), only
  the unit of measurement set out in column I of an item in the
  following table shall be used for offering, advertising or displaying
  gasoline or diesel fuel for retail trade in terms of the price per
  unit of measurement during the period of time set out in column II of
  that item:

  <figure id="534319">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th rowspan="2" class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom" scope="col">Column
  I</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom bottom" scope="col">Column II</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Unit of
  Measurement</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Period of Time</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">gallon</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom top">up to and including December 31,
  1978</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">gallon or litre</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom top">from January 1, 1979 to December 31, 1980
  inclusive</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight top">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight top">litre</td>
  <td class="top">on and after January 1, 1981</td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} On the coming into force of this Part, any retail
  outlet may continue to offer, advertise or display gasoline or diesel
  fuel by the litre if it did so prior to the coming into force of this
  Part.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Up to and including December 31, 1980, where a price
  per unit of measurement of gasoline or diesel fuel is displayed in a
  meter, the half-gallon may be the unit of measurement so displayed in
  the meter if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the price of the commodity exceeds 99.9 cents per
    gallon; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} when the monetary value shown on the meter at the
    end of each transaction is multiplied by a factor of 2, the total
    price to be paid is in mathematical agreement with the quantity
    indication and unit price.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} A retail outlet

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} situated outside a metropolitan government area,
    city, town or other urban municipality, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} serving a sparsely populated area

  may, during 1981 or any year thereafter, continue to

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} offer, advertise or display gasoline or diesel fuel
    for retail trade by the gallon, or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} where a price per unit of measurement of gasoline
    or diesel fuel is displayed in a meter, display in the meter the
    half-gallon as that unit of measurement provided the conditions of
    paragraphs (4)(a) and (b) are met

  if, in the preceding year, total retail sales of gasoline and diesel
  fuel of that retail outlet did not exceed 100,000 gallons.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The mathematical agreement requirement of section 140
  and the unit price display requirement of section 253 do not apply to
  a meter where a price per half-gallon is displayed in the meter and
  the requirements of subsection (4) or (5), whichever is applicable,
  are met.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} Where, pursuant to subsection (4) or (5), whichever
  is applicable, the price displayed in a meter is a price per
  half-gallon, the gallon shall be the unit of measurement used by that
  retail outlet for otherwise offering, advertising or displaying
  gasoline or diesel fuel for retail trade.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-152, s. 1
- SOR/79-390, s. 2(E)
:::

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Retailing of Individually Measured Foods and Scale Conversion]{.HTitleText2} {#h-534340 .Subheading}

- **[[337]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-337 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this Division,

  [[area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a geographic area, as it
      existed on October 1, 1978, described in column I of an item of
      the table to section 341 and includes any city, town, municipality
      or unorganized region geographically situated totally or partially
      in such area; ([régions]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[commodity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[commodity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a commodity in respect of
      which this Division applies; ([marchandise]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[individually measured commodity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[individually measured commodity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the
      meaning set out in section 45. ([marchandise mesurée
      individuellement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} This Division applies in respect of any food that is
  an individually measured commodity, including, without restricting the
  generality of the foregoing, meat, fish, cheese, produce, nuts, candy
  and confections.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-390, s. 3
- SOR/80-83, s. 1
- SOR/81-495, s. 1
:::

- **[[338]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-338
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2) and
  section 340, in each area described in column I of an item of the
  table to section 341, on and after the date set out in column II of
  that item, only metric units of measurement shall be used for
  advertising a commodity for retail trade in terms of price per unit of
  measurement.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In each area described in column I of an item of the
  table to section 341, during the periods commencing on the dates set
  out in column II of that item and ending on December 31, 1985, a
  Canadian unit of measurement may be used for advertising a commodity
  for retail trade in terms of price per unit of measurement if the
  equivalent price per metric unit of measurement is shown in a manner
  more prominent than the Canadian unit of measurement.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-390, s. 3
- SOR/80-83, s. 2
- SOR/81-495, s. 2
- SOR/84-397, s. 1
:::

**[[339]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-339 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 340,
in each area described in column I of an item of the table to section
341, on and after the date set out in column III of that item, no device
shall be used in the retail trade of a commodity unless it is capable of
weighing or measuring in terms of metric units of measurement.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-390, s. 3
- SOR/80-83, s. 2
- SOR/81-495, s. 2
:::

**[[340]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-340 .sectionLabel}** No metric unit of
measurement of mass or weight or any multiple or subdivision thereof
shall be used for offering, advertising or displaying a commodity for
retail trade in terms of price per unit of measurement other than the
unit or subdivision thereof set out in the following expressions:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} price per kilogram; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} price per one hundred grams.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-390, s. 3
:::

**[[341]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-341 .sectionLabel}** Subject to subsection
338(2), in each area described in column I of an item of the following
table, on and after the date set out in column III of that item,
Canadian units of measurement shall not be used in the retail trade of a
commodity:

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Retailing of Home Furnishings]{.HTitleText2} {#h-534371 .Subheading}

**[[342]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-342 .sectionLabel}** In this Division,

[[floor covering]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[floor covering]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any floor covering or
    constituent part thereof for a home, whether partial or
    wall-to-wall, and includes resilient floor coverings, tiles,
    carpets, rugs, undercushions and
    underpaddings; ([couvre-plancher]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[household textile]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[household textile]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any product for use
    in a home made in whole or in part from a textile fibre, yarn or
    fabric, except draperies and curtains and their liners, and includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} such products for use on beds or tables,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} furniture,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} covers for furniture, appliances or bathroom
      fixtures, including throws and slip-covers, and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} towels, dishcloths and bath mats; ([textile de
      maison]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[narrow fabric]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[narrow fabric]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning set out in
    section 2 of the [Textile Labelling and Advertising
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1551); ([article de
    rubanerie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[piece good]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[piece good]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fabric sold by unit of
    measurement, and includes a narrow fabric; ([tissu à la
    pièce]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[textile fibre]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[textile fibre]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning set out in
    section 2 of the [Textile Labelling Act](/eng/acts/T-10). ([fibre
    textile]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-841, s. 1
:::

**[[343]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-343 .sectionLabel}** After December 31,
1979, units of measurement set out in Schedule II to the Act shall not
be used in the retail trade of piece goods.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-841, s. 1
:::

- **[[344]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-344
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2) and
  section 345, after December 31, 1979, units of measurement set out in
  Schedule II to the Act shall not be used in the retail trade of floor
  coverings, household textiles or wallpaper.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 345, after December 31, 1979,
  units of measurement set out in Schedule II to the Act may be used in
  the retail trade of floor coverings, household textiles or wallpaper
  if the equivalent metric units of measurement are shown in a manner at
  least as prominent as the units of measurement set out in Schedule II
  to the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-841, s. 1
:::

- **[[345]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-345
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} During 1980, section 344 does not
  apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a catalogue;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the label of a commodity that prior to January 1,
    1980 is in transit, in a warehouse or received by the retailer; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} advertising outside of retail premises.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} After December 31, 1980, subsection 344(2) does not
  apply to any advertising of floor coverings, household textiles or
  wallpaper.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-841, s. 1
:::

## [PART VIII]{.HLabel1}[Administrative Monetary Penalties]{.HTitleText1} {#h-534400 .Part}

### [Violations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-534401 .Subheading}

**[[346]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-346 .sectionLabel}** The contravention of a
provision of the Act or these Regulations set out in column 1 of Part 1
or 2, respectively, of Schedule VI may be proceeded with as a violation
in accordance with sections 22.1 to 22.28 of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-112, s. 1
:::

### [Classification]{.HTitleText2} {#h-534405 .Subheading}

**[[347]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-347 .sectionLabel}** The violation of each
provision set out in column 1 of Part 1 or 2 of Schedule VI is
classified as a minor, serious or very serious violation in accordance
with the classification set out in column 2.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-112, s. 1
:::

### [Penalties]{.HTitleText2} {#h-534409 .Subheading}

- **[[348]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-348 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  amount of the penalty in respect of each violation is, subject to
  subsection (2),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} \$250, for a minor violation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} \$500, for a serious violation; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} \$1,000, for a very serious violation.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount of the penalty is to be adjusted in
  accordance with the calculation set out in column 2 of Schedule VII
  that corresponds to the history set out in column 1 of the person who
  has committed the violation.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of determining a person's history,
  the points set out in column 3 of Part 1 or 2 of Schedule VI are
  assigned for each violation of a provision set out in column 1.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-112, s. 1
:::

**[[349]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-349 .sectionLabel}** The notice of violation
is to set out that an amount equal to 50% of the penalty may be paid as
complete satisfaction of the penalty if it is paid within the period
referred to in subsection 351(1) and in the manner set out in the notice
of violation in accordance with subsection 351(6).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-112, s. 1
:::

### [Compliance Agreements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-534422 .Subheading}

**[[350]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-350 .sectionLabel}** If the Minister enters
into a compliance agreement under subsection 22.13(1) of the Act, the
conditions of which include the making of expenditures by a person, the
amount of the penalty set out in the notice of violation is to be
reduced by an amount equal to one half of the expenditures made in
fulfilment of the agreement, with a maximum reduction to nil.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-112, s. 1
:::

### [Payment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-534426 .Subheading}

- **[[351]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-351 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of paragraph 22.11(2)(e) of the Act, payment is to be
  made within 15 days after the day on which the person is provided with
  the notice of violation.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 22.12(1) of the Act,
  payment of the penalty set out in the notice of violation is to be
  made within 30 days after the day on which the person is provided with
  the notice.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph 22.13(4)(a) of the Act,
  payment is to be made within 15 days after the day on which the person
  is provided with the notice of default.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 22.14(1) of the Act,
  payment is to be made within 15 days after the day on which the person
  is provided with the Minister's notice.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 22.15(3) of the Act,
  payment is to be made within 15 days after the day on which the person
  is provided with notice of the Minister's decision under subsection
  22.15(1) or (2) of the Act.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} A payment referred to in any of subsections (1)
  to (5) is to be made in one of the following manners, as set out in
  the applicable notice:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by means of a certified cheque or money order made
    payable to the Receiver General for Canada and submitted to a
    Measurement Canada office

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2017-17, s. 18\]]{.Repealed}

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} by registered mail, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} by courier; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by electronic means.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} The date of payment is the day on which it is sent.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-112, s. 1
- SOR/2017-17, s. 18
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-351-20140801.html "Link to previous version of section 351"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Requests Under Subsection 22.12(2) of the Act]{.HTitleText2} {#h-534442 .Subheading}

- **[[352]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-352 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  request under paragraph 22.12(2)(a) or (b) of the Act shall be made,
  as set out in the notice, by submitting the following information in
  writing to a Measurement Canada office in person, by registered mail
  or courier or by electronic means within 30 days after the day on
  which the person is provided with the notice of violation:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the notice of violation number, as set out in the
    notice;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person's name, mailing address, email address,
    telephone number, facsimile number and contact person, if any;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an indication of whether the person prefers to
    communicate in English or French;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a request made under paragraph
    22.12(2)(a) of the Act, a proposal detailing the corrective action
    that will be taken to ensure the person's compliance with the
    provision to which the violation relates; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a request made under paragraph
    22.12(2)(b) of the Act, the person's reasons for requesting the
    review.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2017-17, s. 19\]]{.Repealed}

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The date of the request is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day on which it is submitted, if it is
    submitted in person; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day on which it is sent, if it is sent by
    registered mail, courier or electronic means.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-112, s. 1
- SOR/2017-17, s. 19
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-352-20140801.html "Link to previous version of section 352"){.PITLink}
:::

### [PART II]{.HLabel1}[Formula for Determining the Weighted Average Quantity of the Units in a Sample]{.HTitleText1} {#part-iiformula-for-determining-the-weighted-average-quantity-of-the-units-in-a-sample .SchedHeadL1}

For the purposes of paragraph 52(4)(a), the formula for adjusting the
sample mean to determine the weighted average quantity of the units in
the sample is as follows:

::: {#534605 .Provision}
[X]{.Overbar}~a~ = [x]{.Overbar} + s(t÷√n)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#fn_Ind1BBE_hq_13381){#fn_Ind1BBE_hq_13381-ID0EAAIBFA}

where

[[X]{.Overbar}~a~]{.dfn}
:   is the weighted average quantity of the units in the sample

[[x]{.Overbar}]{.dfn}
:   is the sample mean calculated as follows:
    ::: {#534609 .NestedFormula}
    [x]{.Overbar} = Σx ÷ n
    :::

[Σx]{.dfn}
:   is the sum of the net quantities of all units in the sample

[t]{.dfn}
:   is the value determined in accordance with Part III for the selected
    sample size

[n]{.dfn}
:   is the number of units in the sample

[s]{.dfn}
:   is the standard deviation of the sample, calculated as follows:
    ::: {#534615 .NestedFormula}
    <figure>
    <div class="imageGroupcenter">
    <img src="/images/CRC1605img/CRC1605_e001.jpg"
    alt="s equals the square root of the quotient of the sum of the squared differences of x minus the mean of x divided by the difference of n minus 1" />
    </div>
    </figure>
    :::

[Σ(x-[x]{.Overbar})^2^]{.dfn}
:   is the sum of the squared differences between the sample mean and
    the net quantity of each unit in the sample.
:::

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#fn_Ind1BBE_hq_13381-ID0EAAIBFA){#fn_Ind1BBE_hq_13381}The
  value of (t ÷ √n) may, instead of being calculated in accordance with
  this Part, be determined using the applicable value set out in column
  III of the table to Part III.
  :::

and lies between 32 and 125, the value of t will be determined by linear

<div class="Provision">
<p>t = a - (c-e) ÷ (c-d)(a-b)</p>
<p>where</p>
<dl>
<dt><dfn>a</dfn></dt>
<dd>
is the value of t for the closest sample size below the selected sample
size
</dd>
<dt><dfn>b</dfn></dt>
<dd>
is the value of t for the closest sample size above the selected sample
size
</dd>
<dt><dfn>c</dfn></dt>
<dd>
is the result of 120 divided by the closest sample size below the
selected sample size
</dd>
<dt><dfn>d</dfn></dt>
<dd>
is the result of 120 divided by the closest sample size above the
selected sample size
</dd>
<dt><dfn>e</dfn></dt>
<dd>
is the result of 120 divided divided by the selected size
</dd>

headers="t122h3">1</td>
<td id="t122h7" class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t122h1 t122h4">For the provision by an inspector, in order to
provide a service referred to in subsection 59(1) of these Regulations,
of a piece of equipment or a vehicle set out in any of subitems (a) to
(h), for each hour or part thereof, with the exception of subitem (f),
as follows:</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom bottom" headers="t122h2 t122h5"></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td id="t122h9" class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"
headers="t122h1 t122h4 t122h7">(a) volumetric prover standard, unless
set out in subitems (c), (d) and (e), of</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom bottom" headers="t122h2 t122h5"></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top"
headers="t122h1 t122h4 t122h7 t122h9"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> more than 20 L but less than
2 700 L</p></li>
</ul></td>
<td class="noBorderBottom bottom" headers="t122h2 t122h5">$20.00</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top"
headers="t122h1 t122h4 t122h7 t122h9"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> 2 700 L or more</p></li>
</ul></td>
<td class="noBorderBottom bottom" headers="t122h2 t122h5">$80.00</td>

## [SCHEDULE VII]{.scheduleLabel}[(Subsection 348(2))]{.OriginatingRef} {#h-534808 .scheduleLabel}
:::

<figure id="534809">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom"></th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">History</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Adjustment Calculation</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">No violations committed
under the Act or these Regulations in the five-year period immediately
before the day on which the current violation was committed.</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Reduce penalty by 50%</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Sum of the points assigned
for all violations committed under the Act or these Regulations in the
five-year period immediately before the day on which the current
violation was committed is either one or two.</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Reduce penalty by 25%</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Sum of the points assigned
for all violations committed under the Act or these Regulations in the
five-year period immediately before the day on which the current
violation was committed is not less than three and not more than
five.</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">No penalty adjustment</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">4</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Sum of the points assigned
for all violations committed under the Act or these Regulations in the
five-year period immediately before the day on which the current
violation was committed is not less than six and not more than
eight.</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">Increase penalty by 25%</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight top">5</td>
<td class="noBorderRight top">Sum of the points assigned for all
violations committed under the Act or these Regulations in the five-year
period immediately before the day on which the current violation was
committed is more than eight.</td>
<td class="top">Increase penalty by 50%</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>Penalty Adjustments</p></figcaption>
</figure>
